23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-06 · #24
Considerable work by cultural psychologists and anthropologists has shown that there are indeed large and sometimes surprising differences in the words and concepts that different cultures have for describing emotions, as well as in the social circumstances that draw out the expression of particular emotions. 역접However, those data do not actually show that different cultures have different emotions, if we think of emotions as central, neurally implemented states. As for, say, color vision, they just say that, 양보despite the same internal processing architecture, how we interpret, categorize, and name emotions varies according to culture and that we learn in a particular culture the social context in which it is appropriate to express emotions. 역접However, the emotional states themselves are likely to be quite invariant across cultures. In a sense, we can think of a basic, culturally universal emotion set that is shaped by evolution and implemented in the brain, 대조but the links between such emotional states and stimuli, behavior, and other cognitive states are plastic and can be modified by learning in a specific cultural context.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①문화마다 감정을 기술하는 말·개념은 크게 다르다 (도입 자료) — — 단, 이것이 곧 '감정 자체가 다름'을 뜻하진 않는다(However)
  2. 노랑②같은 처리 구조에도 감정을 해석·범주화·명명하는 방식은 문화 따라 다르다 — — 문화가 바꾸는 것은 감정의 '표상(표현·이름)'
  3. 노랑③그러나 감정 상태 자체는 문화 간에 거의 불변이다 — — 감정=진화적·보편적, 문화가 만드는 것은 그 표상
→ 문화가 구성하는 것은 감정 자체가 아니라 감정의 '표상' = ② culturally constructed representation of emotions
첫 문장(자료 소개)은 도입이라 gray. 판정은 However 두 문장으로 충분.
패러프레이즈 how we interpret, categorize, and name emotions varies according to culture → culturally constructed representation of emotions ✓ / 복사 함정 behaviors links
①emotions and behaviors 사이의 본질적 연결✘ 표면어휘
②감정의 문화적으로 구성된 표상✔ 근거
③세계 언어로 잘못 기술된 감정✘ 없음
④학문 분야를 넘어 보편 정의된 감정✘ 없음
⑤인지가 문화 맥락 학습에 미치는 넓은 영향✘ 범위초과
공식반복 키워드 + 주제문 → 추상 선지
23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
how we interpret, categorize, and name emotions varies according to culture, despite the same internal processing architecture.
우리가 감정을 해석·범주화·명명하는 방식은 문화에 따라 달라진다, 동일한 내부 처리 구조에도 불구하고. ← 근거
2
However, the emotional states themselves are likely to be quite invariant across cultures.
그러나, 감정 상태 그 자체는 문화 간에 거의 변하지 않을 것이다. ← 근거
②정답 감정의 문화적으로 구성된 표상(해석·범주화·명명) · ①behaviors 표면어휘 · ③④근거 없음 · ⑤인지 영향(범위초과)

23주제 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Managers of natural resources typically face market incentives that provide financial rewards for exploitation. For example, owners of forest lands have a market incentive to cut down trees rather than manage the forest for carbon capture, wildlife habitat, flood protection, and other ecosystem services. These services provide the owner with no financial benefits, and thus are unlikely to influence management decisions. But the economic benefits provided by these services, based on their non-market values, may exceed the economic value of the timber. For example, a United Nations initiative has estimated that the economic benefits of ecosystem services provided by tropical forests, including climate regulation, water purification, and erosion prevention, are over three times greater per hectare than the market benefits. Thus cutting down the trees is economically inefficient, and markets are not sending the correct “signal” to favor ecosystem services over extractive uses.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① necessity of calculating the market values of ecosystem services② impact of using forest resources to maximize financial benefits③ ways of increasing the efficiency of managing natural resources④ significance of weighing forest resources’ non-market values⑤ merits of balancing forests’ market and non-market values

1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지

23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #25
Managers of natural resources typically face market incentives that provide financial rewards for exploitation. For example, owners of forest lands have a market incentive to cut down trees rather than manage the forest for carbon capture, wildlife habitat, flood protection, and other ecosystem services. These services provide the owner with no financial benefits, and 인과thus are unlikely to influence management decisions. 역접But the economic benefits provided by these services, based on their non-market values, may exceed the economic value of the timber. For example, a United Nations initiative has estimated that the economic benefits of ecosystem services provided by tropical forests, including climate regulation, water purification, and erosion prevention, are over three times greater per hectare than the market benefits. 결론Thus cutting down the trees is economically inefficient, and markets are not sending the correct “signal” to favor ecosystem services over extractive uses.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①But 비시장 가치가 목재 가치를 넘어설 수 있다 — — 역접이 핵심 주장
  2. 노랑②Thus 나무 베기는 비효율, 시장 신호가 틀림 — — 결론
→ 산림의 비시장 가치를 따지는 것이 중요하다 = ④
회색(도입·UN 수치 예시)은 근거 보강일 뿐 빼도 무방.
패러프레이즈 non-market values may exceed · economically inefficient → significance of weighing non-market values ✓ / 복사 함정 market values
①생태계 서비스의 market values 계산의 필요성✘ 정반대
②재정 이익 극대화를 위한 산림 자원 이용의 영향✘ 범위초과
③천연자원 관리 효율을 높이는 방법✘ 없음
④산림 자원의 비시장 가치를 따지는 것의 중요성✔ 근거
⑤산림의 시장·비시장 가치의 균형 이점✘ 범위초과
공식반복 키워드 + 주제문 → 추상 선지
23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
But the economic benefits provided by these services, based on their non-market values, may exceed the economic value of the timber.
하지만 이 서비스들이 제공하는 경제적 이익은, 그것들의 비시장 가치에 근거해, 목재의 경제적 가치를 넘어설 수 있다. ← 근거
2
Thus cutting down the trees is economically inefficient, and markets are not sending the correct “signal” to favor ecosystem services over extractive uses.
따라서 나무를 베는 것은 경제적으로 비효율적이며, 시장은 올바른 '신호'를 보내지 않는다 채취적 이용보다 생태계 서비스를 선호하도록 하는. ← 근거
④정답 산림의 비시장 가치를 따지는 것의 중요성 · ①시장가치 계산 ②재정이익 극대화 ③관리 효율 ⑤둘의 균형

23주제 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
An important advantage of disclosure, as opposed to more aggressive forms of regulation, is its flexibility and respect for the operation of free markets. Regulatory mandates are blunt swords; they tend to neglect diversity and may have serious unintended adverse effects. For example, energy efficiency requirements for appliances may produce goods that work less well or that have characteristics that consumers do not want. Information provision, by contrast, respects freedom of choice. If automobile manufacturers are required to measure and publicize the safety characteristics of cars, potential car purchasers can trade safety concerns against other attributes, such as price and styling. If restaurant customers are informed of the calories in their meals, those who want to lose weight can make use of the information, leaving those who are unconcerned about calories unaffected. Disclosure does not interfere with, and should even promote, the autonomy (and quality) of individual decision-making.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① steps to make public information accessible to customers② benefits of publicizing information to ensure free choices③ strategies for companies to increase profits in a free market④ necessities of identifying and analyzing current industry trends⑤ effects of diversified markets on reasonable customer choices

2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지

Disclosure does not interfere with, and 강조should even promote, the autonomy (and quality) of individual decision-making.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①핵심①: 정보 공개(disclosure)의 이점은 규제와 달리 자유 시장 존중과 유연성 — — 화제: 정보 공개의 이점(vs 규제)
  2. 노랑②핵심②: Information provision은 대조적으로 선택의 자유를 존중 — — 안전·칼로리 공개 예시로 선택권 보장
  3. 노랑③핵심③: 정보 공개는 개인 의사결정의 자율성을 촉진 — — 자유로운 선택 보장이라는 이점으로 결론
→ 자유로운 선택을 보장하는 정보 공개의 이점 = ② benefits of publicizing information to ensure free choices
자동차·식당 예시(회색)는 근거를 예시할 뿐 주제가 아님.
패러프레이즈 advantage of disclosure / respects freedom of choice → benefits of publicizing information to ensure free choices ✓ / 복사 함정 information free market diversity
①steps to make public information accessible to customers✘ 없음
②benefits of publicizing information to ensure free choices✔ 근거
③strategies for companies to increase profits in a free market✘ 없음
④necessities of identifying and analyzing current industry trends✘ 없음
⑤effects of diversified markets on reasonable customer choices✘ 범위초과
공식반복 키워드 + 주제문 → 추상 선지
23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
An important advantage of disclosure, as opposed to more aggressive forms of regulation, is its flexibility and respect for the operation of free markets.
정보 공개의 중요한 이점은, 더 공격적인 형태의 규제와 대조적으로, 그 유연성과 자유 시장 작동에 대한 존중이다. ← 근거
2
Disclosure does not interfere with, and should even promote, the autonomy of individual decision-making.
정보 공개는 방해하지 않으며, 오히려 촉진해야 한다, 개인 의사결정의 자율성을. ← 근거
②정답 자유로운 선택을 보장하는 정보 공개의 이점 · ①접근 절차 ③이윤 전략 ④산업 동향 분석 ⑤다양화 시장 효과
wbspread
23주제 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
In writing a life, the life narrator and the biographer engage different kinds of evidence. Most biographers incorporate multiple forms of evidence, including historical documents, interviews, and family archives, which they evaluate for validity. Relatively few biographers use their personal memories of their subject as reliable evidence, unless they had a personal relationship to the subject of the biography (as a relative, child, friend, or colleague). For life narrators, by contrast, personal memories are the primary archival source. They may have recourse to other kinds of sources ― letters, journals, photographs, conversations ― and to their knowledge of a historical moment. But the usefulness of such evidence for their stories lies in the ways in which they employ that evidence to support, supplement, or offer commentary on their personalized acts of remembering. In autobiographical narratives, imaginative acts of remembering always overlap with such rhetorical acts as assertion, justification, judgment, conviction, and questioning.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① role of personal memories in different types of life storytelling② advantages of documenting evidence-based family histories③ influence of the source types on the quality of life narratives④ similarity of data sources in describing a person’s life⑤ crucial conditions for the success of life narrators

1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지
📢 신호 리마인더
역접 However결론 Therefore반복 키워드예시 such as ✕

23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2026-09 · #27
In writing a life, the life narrator and the biographer engage 대조different kinds of evidence. Most biographers incorporate multiple forms of evidence, including historical documents, interviews, and family archives, which they evaluate for validity. 한정 Relatively few biographers use their personal memories of their subject as reliable evidence, unless they had a personal relationship to the subject of the biography (as a relative, child, friend, or colleague). For life narrators, 역접대조by contrast, personal memories are the primary archival source. They may have recourse to other kinds of sources ― letters, journals, photographs, conversations ― and to their knowledge of a historical moment. 역접But the usefulness of such evidence for their stories lies in the ways in which they employ that evidence to support, supplement, or offer commentary on their personalized acts of remembering. In autobiographical narratives, imaginative acts of remembering always overlap with such rhetorical acts as assertion, justification, judgment, conviction, and questioning.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①전기 작가와 자기 서술자는 다른 종류의 증거를 다룬다 — — 글의 축: 개인적 기억의 위상 차이
  2. 노랑②전기 작가: 개인적 기억은 거의 안 씀 ↔ 서술자: 개인적 기억이 주요 자료 — — by contrast로 정반대 대비
  3. 노랑③서술자에게 다른 증거의 유용성도 결국 개인적 기억을 뒷받침하는 데 있다 — — But으로 개인적 기억 중심성 강조
→ 세 문장을 이으면: 삶을 이야기하는 방식별로 개인적 기억이 하는 역할 = ①
회색(전기 작가의 자료 종류 나열)은 대비를 세우는 배경일 뿐, 주제어 아님.
패러프레이즈 different kinds of evidence · personal memories are the primary source → role of personal memories in different types of life storytelling ✓ / 복사 함정 evidence source similarity

23주제 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
There are pressures within the museum that cause it to emphasise what happens in the galleries over the activities that take place in its unseen zones. In an era when museums are forced to increase their earnings, they often focus their energies on modernising their galleries or mounting temporary exhibitions to bring more and more audiences through the door. In other words, as museums struggle to survive in a competitive economy, their budgets often prioritise those parts of themselves that are consumable: infotainment in the galleries, goods and services in the cafes and the shops. The unlit, unglamorous storerooms, if they are ever discussed, are at best presented as service areas that process objects for the exhibition halls. And at worst, as museums pour more and more resources into their publicly visible faces, the spaces of storage may even suffer, their modernisation being kept on hold or being given less and less space to house the expanding collections and serve their complex conservation needs.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① importance of prioritising museums’ exhibition spaces② benefits of diverse activities in museums for audiences③ necessity of expanding storerooms for displaying objects④ consequences of profit-oriented management of museums⑤ ways to increase museums’ commitment to the public good

23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-06 · #28
핵심어 There are pressures within the museum that cause it to emphasise what happens in the galleries over the activities that take place in its unseen zones. In an era when museums are 조건forced to increase their earnings, they often focus their energies on modernising their galleries or mounting temporary exhibitions to bring more and more audiences through the door. 환언In other words, as museums struggle to survive in a competitive economy, their budgets often prioritise those parts of themselves that are consumable: infotainment in the galleries, goods and services in the cafes and the shops. The unlit, unglamorous storerooms, if they are ever discussed, are 양보at best presented as service areas that process objects for the exhibition halls. 결과 And at worst, as museums pour more and more resources into their publicly visible faces, the spaces of storage may even suffer, their modernisation being kept on hold or being given less and less space to house the expanding collections and serve their complex conservation needs.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①박물관 내부엔 보이지 않는 구역보다 전시실을 강조하게 만드는 압력이 있다 — — 수익 압력이 우선순위를 왜곡
  2. 노랑②수익을 늘려야 하는 시대, 예산은 소비 가능한 부분(전시·카페·상점)에 우선 배정 — — 이익 지향 운영의 구체상
  3. 노랑③최악의 경우, 보이는 얼굴에 자원을 쏟느라 수장고가 오히려 피해를 본다 — — 이익 지향 운영의 결과·대가
→ 이익 지향 박물관 운영이 낳는 결과(보존 공간의 희생) = ④
회색(at best 수장고를 서비스 구역으로 보는 시각)은 대비를 위한 양보일 뿐.
패러프레이즈 prioritise consumable parts · storage may suffer → consequences of profit-oriented management ✓ / 복사 함정 exhibition spaces storerooms activities public good
④consequences of profit-oriented management of museums✔ 근거

23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
as museums struggle to survive in a competitive economy, their budgets often prioritise those parts of themselves that are consumable.
박물관이 경쟁 경제에서 살아남으려 애쓰면서, 예산은 흔히 우선시한다 소비 가능한 부분들을. ← 근거(이익 지향 운영)
2
as museums pour more and more resources into their publicly visible faces, the spaces of storage may even suffer, their modernisation being kept on hold.
박물관이 대중에 보이는 얼굴에 자원을 점점 더 쏟으면서, 수장 공간은 오히려 피해를 볼 수 있고, 그 현대화는 보류된다. ← 근거(운영의 결과)
④정답 이익 지향 운영의 결과 · ①전시공간 우선의 중요성(정반대) ②다양한 활동의 이익 ③수장고 확장 필요 ⑤공익 기여 방법

23주제 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Scientists use paradigms rather than believing them. The use of a paradigm in research typically addresses related problems by employing shared concepts, symbolic expressions, experimental and mathematical tools and procedures, and even some of the same theoretical statements. Scientists need only understand how to use these various elements in ways that others would accept. These elements of shared practice thus need not presuppose any comparable unity in scientists’ beliefs about what they are doing when they use them. Indeed, one role of a paradigm is to enable scientists to work successfully without having to provide a detailed account of what they are doing or what they believe about it. Thomas Kuhn noted that scientists “can agree in their identification of a paradigm without agreeing on, or even attempting to produce, a full interpretation or rationalization of it. Lack of a standard interpretation or of an agreed reduction to rules will not prevent a paradigm from guiding research.”
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① difficulty in drawing novel theories from existing paradigms② significant influence of personal beliefs in scientific fields③ key factors that promote the rise of innovative paradigms④ roles of a paradigm in grouping like-minded researchers⑤ functional aspects of a paradigm in scientific research

3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지

23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2022-수능 · #29 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
Scientists use paradigms 대조rather than believing them. The use of a paradigm in research typically addresses related problems by employing shared concepts, symbolic expressions, experimental and mathematical tools and procedures, and even some of the same theoretical statements. Scientists need 한정only understand how to use these various elements in ways that others would accept. These elements of shared practice 결론thus need not presuppose any comparable unity in scientists’ beliefs about what they are doing when they use them. 강조Indeed, one role of a paradigm is to enable scientists to work successfully without having to provide a detailed account of what they are doing or what they believe about it. Thomas Kuhn noted that scientists “can agree in their identification of a paradigm without agreeing on, or even attempting to produce, a full interpretation or rationalization of it. Lack of a standard interpretation or of an agreed reduction to rules will not prevent a paradigm from guiding research.”
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①과학자는 패러다임을 믿는 게 아니라 사용한다 — — 초점은 신념이 아니라 '쓰임/기능'
  2. 노랑②공유된 실천은 신념의 통일성을 전제하지 않는다 — — 믿음 없이도 작동
  3. 노랑③패러다임의 한 역할: 상세한 설명 없이 연구를 성공시킴 — — 패러다임이 하는 '기능' 서술
→ 세 문장을 이으면: 패러다임의 과학 연구에서의 기능적 측면 = ⑤
예시(개념·기호 나열)와 Kuhn 인용(회색)은 빼도 논리에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 use·role·enable to work without shared belief → functional aspects of a paradigm ✓ / 복사 함정 roles beliefs novel theories
⑤functional aspects of a paradigm in scientific research✔ 근거
①difficulty in drawing novel theories from paradigms✘ 없음
②significant influence of personal beliefs✘ 정반대
③key factors that promote the rise of innovative paradigms✘ 없음
④roles of a paradigm in grouping like-minded researchers✘ 범위초과

23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Scientists use paradigms rather than believing them.
과학자들은 패러다임을 사용한다 그것을 믿는 것이 아니라. ← 근거
2
These elements of shared practice thus need not presuppose any comparable unity in scientists’ beliefs.
이 공유된 실천의 요소들은 따라서 전제할 필요가 없다 과학자들의 믿음에서의 어떤 상응하는 통일성도. ← 근거
3
Indeed, one role of a paradigm is to enable scientists to work successfully without providing a detailed account of what they believe.
실제로, 패러다임의 한 역할은 과학자들이 성공적으로 연구하게 하는 것이다 자신이 믿는 바에 대한 상세한 설명 없이도. ← 근거
⑤정답 과학 연구에서 패러다임의 기능적 측면 · ①새 이론 도출 어려움 ②개인 신념 영향(정반대) ③혁신 패러다임 등장 요인 ④연구자 결집 역할(범위초과)

23주제 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Children can move effortlessly between play and absorption in a story, as if both are forms of the same activity. The taking of roles in a narratively structured game of pirates is not very different than the taking of roles in identifying with characters as one watches a movie. It might be thought that, as they grow towards adolescence, people give up childhood play, but this is not so. Instead, the bases and interests of this activity change and develop to playing and watching sports, to the fiction of plays, novels, and movies, and nowadays to video games. In fiction, one can enter possible worlds. When we experience emotions in such worlds, this is not a sign that we are being incoherent or regressed. It derives from trying out metaphorical transformations of our selves in new ways, in new worlds, in ways that can be moving and important to us.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① relationship between play types and emotional stability② reasons for identifying with imaginary characters in childhood③ ways of helping adolescents develop good reading habits④ continued engagement in altered forms of play after childhood⑤ effects of narrative structures on readers’ imaginations
🖍 이렇게 풀어요
1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지

23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2022-06 · #30 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
Children can move effortlessly between play and absorption in a story, as if both are forms of the 주제same activity. The taking of roles in a narratively structured game of pirates is not very different than the taking of roles in identifying with characters as one watches a movie. It might be thought that, as they grow towards adolescence, people give up childhood play, 역접but this is 부정not so. 대조Instead, the bases and interests of this activity change and develop to playing and watching sports, to the fiction of plays, novels, and movies, and nowadays to video games. In fiction, one can enter possible worlds. When we experience emotions in such worlds, this is not a sign that we are being incoherent or regressed. It derives from trying out metaphorical transformations of our selves in new ways, in new worlds, in ways that can be moving and important to us.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①아이는 놀이와 이야기 몰입을 '같은 활동'처럼 오간다 — — 놀이=이야기 몰입
  2. 노랑②청소년이 되면 놀이를 그만둘 것 같지만 '그렇지 않다(not so)' — — but으로 통념 반박
  3. 노랑③대신(Instead) 그 활동의 토대가 스포츠·소설·영화·비디오게임으로 '변화·발전'한다 — — 형태만 바뀌어 지속
→ 놀이는 유년 이후에도 형태를 바꿔 계속된다 = ④
pirates·감정 경험 문장은 예시·부연—스킵해도 논리 성립.
패러프레이즈 give up childhood play … but not so · change and develop to sports/fiction/video games → continued engagement in altered forms of play ✓ / 복사 함정 play identifying with narrative structures
④continued engagement in altered forms of play after childhood✔ 근거
①relationship between play types and emotional stability✘ 범위초과
②reasons for identifying with imaginary characters in childhood✘ 범위초과
③helping adolescents develop good reading habits✘ 없음
⑤effects of narrative structures on readers' imaginations✘ 표면어휘
공식반복 키워드 + 주제문 → 추상 선지
23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
It might be thought that people give up childhood play, but this is not so.
~라고 생각될 수도 있다 사람들이 유년기 놀이를 그만둔다고, 하지만 그렇지 않다. ← 근거
2
Instead, the bases and interests of this activity change and develop to sports, to the fiction of plays, novels, and movies, and nowadays to video games.
대신, 이 활동의 토대와 관심은 변화하고 발전한다 스포츠로, 연극·소설·영화의 허구로, 그리고 오늘날에는 비디오게임으로. ← 근거
④정답 유년 이후 형태를 바꿔 지속되는 놀이 참여 · ①놀이유형과 정서 안정 ②유년기 캐릭터 동일시 이유 ③청소년 독서 습관 ⑤서사 구조가 상상에 미치는 영향

23주제 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
The primary purpose of commercial music radio broadcasting is to deliver an audience to a group of advertisers and sponsors. To achieve commercial success, that audience must be as large as possible. More than any other characteristics (such as demographic or psychographic profile, purchasing power, level of interest, degree of satisfaction, quality of attention or emotional state), the quantity of an audience aggregated as a mass is the most significant metric for broadcasters seeking to make music radio for profitable ends. As a result, broadcasters attempt to maximise their audience size by playing music that is popular, or ― at the very least ― music that can be relied upon not to cause audiences to switch off their radio or change the station. Audience retention is a key value (if not the key value) for many music programmers and for radio station management. In consequence, a high degree of risk aversion frequently marks out the ‘successful’ radio music programmer. Playlists are restricted, and often very small.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① features of music playlists appealing to international audiences② influence of advertisers on radio audiences’ musical preferences③ difficulties of increasing audience size in radio music programmes④ necessity of satisfying listeners’ diverse needs in the radio business⑤ outcome of music radio businesses’ attempts to attract large audiences

The 핵심어primary purpose of commercial music radio broadcasting is to deliver an audience to a group of advertisers and sponsors. To achieve commercial success, that audience 당위must be as large as possible. More than any other characteristics (such as demographic or psychographic profile, purchasing power, level of interest, degree of satisfaction, quality of attention or emotional state), the quantity of an audience aggregated as a mass is 최상급the most significant metric for broadcasters seeking to make music radio for profitable ends. 인과As a result, broadcasters attempt to maximise their audience size by playing music that is popular, or ― at the very least ― music that can be relied upon not to cause audiences to switch off their radio or change the station. Audience retention is a 핵심어key value (if not the key value) for many music programmers and for radio station management. 인과In consequence, a high degree of risk aversion frequently marks out the ‘successful’ radio music programmer. Playlists are 결과restricted, and often very small.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①상업적 성공엔 청중이 가능한 한 커야 하고, '대중의 규모(양)'가 가장 중요한 지표 — — 목표: 최대 청중 확보
  2. 노랑②그 결과 방송사는 인기곡·안전한 곡을 틀어 청중 규모를 극대화 — — 목표가 낳는 행동
  3. 노랑③그 결과 위험 회피 성향이 강해지고 → 재생목록이 제한적이고 매우 작아짐 — — 최종 귀결(결과)
→ 대규모 청중을 끌려는 시도가 낳는 '결과(안전 위주·제한된 선곡)' = ⑤
청중 유지가 핵심 가치라는 문장은 인과 사슬의 연결 고리라 주제 압축엔 부차적.
패러프레이즈 maximise audience size · risk aversion · playlists restricted → outcome of attempts to attract large audiences ✓ / 복사 함정 international advertisers difficulties diverse needs
⑤outcome of attempts to attract large audiences✔ 근거
①playlists appealing to international audiences✘ 없음
②influence of advertisers on musical preferences✘ 범위초과

23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
the quantity of an audience aggregated as a mass is the most significant metric for broadcasters seeking to make music radio for profitable ends.
대중으로 모인 청중의 양(규모)이 가장 중요한 지표이다 방송사에게 수익을 위해 음악 라디오를 만들려는. ← 목표=최대 청중
2
As a result, broadcasters attempt to maximise their audience size by playing music that is popular, or ― at the very least ― music that can be relied upon not to cause audiences to switch off.
그 결과, 방송사는 청중 규모를 극대화하려 한다 인기 있는 음악을 틀어서, 또는 ― 최소한 ― 음악을 청중이 라디오를 끄지 않게 믿을 수 있는. ← 근거(시도의 결과)
3
In consequence, a high degree of risk aversion frequently marks out the ‘successful’ programmer. Playlists are restricted, and often very small.
그 결과, 높은 정도의 위험 회피가 '성공한' 프로그래머를 자주 특징짓는다. 재생목록은 제한되고, 흔히 매우 작다. ← 근거(최종 귀결)
⑤정답 대규모 청중 확보 시도 → 안전 위주·제한된 선곡이라는 '결과' · ①국제청중 없음 ②선호 조종 아님 ③어려움 아님 ④다양성 충족은 정반대

23주제 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
While many city shoppers were clearly drawn to the notion of buying and eating foods associated with nature, the nature claimed by the ads was no longer the nature that created the foods. Indeed, the nature claimed by many ads was associated with food products only by the ads’ attachment. This is clearly a case of what French sociologist Henri Lefebvre has called “the decline of the referentials,” or the tendency of words under the influence of capitalism to become separated from meaningful associations. Increasingly, food ads helped shoppers become accustomed to new definitions of words such as “fresh” and “natural,” definitions that could well be considered opposite of their traditional meanings. The new definitions better served the needs of the emerging industrial food system, which could not supply foods that matched customary meanings and expectations. And they better met shoppers’ desires, although with pretense.
다음 글의 주제로 가장 적절한 것은?
① decline of reliability in the ads of natural foods② changes in the senses of words linked to food ads③ influence of capitalism on the industrial food system④ various ways to attract customers in the food industry⑤ necessity of meaningful word associations in commercials

23주제STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2025-06 · #32 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
While many city shoppers were clearly drawn to the notion of buying and eating foods associated with nature, the nature claimed by the ads was 대조no longer the nature that created the foods. 강조Indeed, the nature claimed by many ads was associated with food products only by the ads’ attachment. 명명·개념This is clearly a case of what French sociologist Henri Lefebvre has called “the decline of the referentials,” or the tendency of words under the influence of capitalism to become separated from meaningful associations. Increasingly, food ads helped shoppers become accustomed to new definitions of words such as “fresh” and “natural,” definitions that could well be considered 대조opposite of their traditional meanings. The new definitions better served the needs of the emerging industrial food system, which could not supply foods that matched customary meanings and expectations. And they better met shoppers’ desires, although with pretense.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①광고가 내세운 ‘자연’은 더 이상 그 음식을 만든 자연이 아니다 — — 단어가 실제 의미와 분리됨
  2. 노랑②이것은 Lefebvre의 ‘지시체의 쇠퇴’ = 자본주의 아래 단어가 의미 연상과 분리되는 경향 — — 핵심 = 단어 의미의 변화
  3. 노랑③‘fresh’·‘natural’ 같은 단어가 전통적 뜻과 반대인 새 정의로 바뀐다 — — 식품 광고 속 단어 의미의 변화
→ 식품 광고와 연결된 단어들의 의미 변화 = ②
산업·소비자 욕구 언급(회색)은 부연일 뿐.
패러프레이즈 the decline of the referentials·new definitions opposite of traditional meanings → changes in the senses of words ✓ / 복사 함정 capitalism industrial food system decline
①decline of reliability in ads of natural foods✘ 표면어휘
②changes in the senses of words linked to food ads✔ 근거

④various ways to attract customers in the food industry✘ 없음

23주제STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
This is a case of “the decline of the referentials,” words separated from meaningful associations.
이것은 ~의 사례다 ‘지시체의 쇠퇴’, 단어가 의미 있는 연상과 분리되는 것. ← 개념 근거
2
Food ads helped shoppers become accustomed to new definitions of “fresh” and “natural,” opposite of their traditional meanings.
식품 광고는 소비자가 ‘신선한’·‘자연의’ 같은 단어의 새 정의에 익숙해지게 했고, 그 전통적 의미와는 반대인. ← 근거
②정답 식품 광고와 연결된 단어들의 의미 변화 · ①신뢰성 쇠퇴 ③자본주의→산업식품 ④고객 유인법 ⑤의미연상 필요성

유형 04 / 08 PART 1 · 유형별 훈련

제목
24번대 · 실제 기출 11문항
유형 공식주제문을 압축·비유한 선지
이렇게 접근한다주제문을 한 번 더 압축·비유한 것이 제목. 너무 좁거나 너무 넓으면 오답이다.
STEP 1직접 풀기신호를 떠올리며 직접 형광펜을 치고 답을 고른다 (왼쪽 페이지)
STEP 2훈련 · 정답 칠🟡읽을 문장·🔴칠한 근거 + 노랑만으로 정답 도출 + 선지 판정
STEP 3직독직해같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 청크 대응 (오른쪽 페이지)
📢 이 유형 신호 리마인더
역접 However결론 In sum주장 must예시 e.g. ✕
wbspread
24제목 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Different parts of the brain’s visual system get information on a need-to-know basis. Cells that help your hand muscles reach out to an object need to know the size and location of the object, but they don’t need to know about color. They need to know a little about shape, but not in great detail. Cells that help you recognize people’s faces need to be extremely sensitive to details of shape, but they can pay less attention to location. It is natural to assume that anyone who sees an object sees everything about it ― the shape, color, location, and movement. However, one part of your brain sees its shape, another sees color, another detects location, and another perceives movement. Consequently, after localized brain damage, it is possible to see certain aspects of an object and not others. Centuries ago, people found it difficult to imagine how someone could see an object without seeing what color it is. Even today, you might find it surprising to learn about people who see an object without seeing where it is, or see it without seeing whether it is moving.
다음 글의 제목으로 가장 적절한 것은?

24제목STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-수능 · #33 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
Different parts of the brain’s visual system get information on a need-to-know basis. Cells that help your hand muscles reach out to an object need to know the size and location of the object, but they don’t need to know about color. They need to know a little about shape, but not in great detail. Cells that help you recognize people’s faces need to be extremely sensitive to details of shape, but they can pay less attention to location. It is natural to assume that anyone who sees an object sees everything about it ― the shape, color, location, and movement. 역접However, one part of your brain sees its shape, another sees color, another detects location, and another perceives movement. 결과Consequently, after localized brain damage, it is possible to see certain aspects of an object and not others. Centuries ago, people found it difficult to imagine how someone could see an object without seeing what color it is. Even today, you might find it surprising to learn about people who see an object without seeing where it is, or see it without seeing whether it is moving.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①뇌의 시각 시스템의 서로 다른 부분이 필요한 정보만 받는다 — — '분업' 신호로 시작
  2. 노랑②그러나 한 부분은 형태를, 다른 부분은 색을, 또 다른 부분은 위치·움직임을 본다 — — 각 요소를 별개 부위가 처리
  3. 노랑③따라서 국소 뇌 손상 후 어떤 측면은 보고 다른 측면은 못 볼 수 있다 — — 분리·독립되어 있다는 증거
→ 세 문장을 이으면: 뇌 세포의 시지각은 분리되고 독립적이다 = ⑤
회색·회색(손·얼굴 세포 예시, 역사 일화)은 분업을 보여주는 예시일 뿐.
패러프레이즈 different parts · another sees ... · see certain aspects and not others → Separate and Independent ✓ / 복사 함정 Color

24제목STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Different parts of the brain’s visual system get information on a need-to-know basis.
뇌 시각 시스템의 서로 다른 부분들은 필요한 것만 아는 방식으로 정보를 얻는다. ← 근거
2
However, one part of your brain sees its shape, another sees color, another detects location, and another perceives movement.
그러나 뇌의 한 부분은 형태를 보고, 다른 부분은 색을, 또 다른 부분은 위치를 감지하며, 또 다른 부분은 움직임을 인지한다. ← 근거
3
Consequently, after localized brain damage, it is possible to see certain aspects of an object and not others.
따라서 국소적 뇌 손상 후에는, 어떤 측면은 보면서 다른 측면은 못 볼 수 있다. ← 근거
⑤정답 분리되고 독립적인 뇌세포의 시지각 · ①시각은 신뢰를 배신 안 함(정반대) ②색 민감 세포의 비밀 임무 ③뇌의 미지 영역 ④자연의 회복 과정

24제목 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
The concept of overtourism rests on a particular assumption about people and places common in tourism studies and the social sciences in general. Both are seen as clearly defined and demarcated. People are framed as bounded social actors either playing the role of hosts or guests. Places, in a similar way, are treated as stable containers with clear boundaries. Hence, places can be full of tourists and thus suffer from overtourism. But what does it mean for a place to be full of people? Indeed, there are examples of particular attractions that have limited capacity and where there is actually no room for more visitors. This is not least the case with some man-made constructions such as the Eiffel Tower. However, with places such as cities, regions or even whole countries being promoted as destinations and described as victims of overtourism, things become more complex. What is excessive or out of proportion is highly relative and might be more related to other aspects than physical capacity, such as natural degradation and economic leakages (not to mention politics and local power dynamics).
다음 글의 제목으로 가장 적절한 것은?

24제목STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #34
The concept of overtourism rests on a particular assumption about people and places common in tourism studies and the social sciences in general. Both are seen as clearly defined and demarcated. 🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①오버투어리즘은 '사람·장소가 경계 뚜렷한 그릇'이라는 가정에 기댐 → 그래서 '가득 참' — — 통념(전제) 제시
  2. 노랑②However 도시·지역·나라 단위가 관광지가 되면 문제가 더 복잡 — — 역접: 단순한 물리적 포화론이 무너짐
  3. 노랑③'과도함'은 매우 상대적, 물리적 수용력보다 환경훼손·경제유출 등과 관련 — — 결론: 단순한 사람·장소 문제가 아님
→ 오버투어리즘은 단순히 사람·장소(물리적 포화)의 문제가 아니다 = ①
hosts/guests·에펠탑 등 예시(Indeed·such as)는 부연이라 빼도 됨.

24제목STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응

The concept of overtourism rests on a particular assumption about people and places.

2

그러나 도시·지역, 심지어 나라 전체 같은 장소가 관광지로 홍보되고 오버투어리즘의 피해자로 묘사되면, 문제는 더 복잡해진다. ← 근거
3
What is excessive or out of proportion is highly relative and might be more related to other aspects than physical capacity.
무엇이 과도하거나 균형을 벗어난 것인지는 매우 상대적이며 물리적 수용력보다 다른 측면과 더 관련될 수 있다. ← 근거
①정답 오버투어리즘은 단순히 사람·장소의 문제가 아님 · ②심각성 과소평가 ③복잡→단순 해법 ④명소는 승자/패자? ⑤인기 관광지의 매력 요인

24제목 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Hyper-mobility ― the notion that more travel at faster speeds covering longer distances generates greater economic success ― seems to be a distinguishing feature of urban areas, where more than half of the world’s population currently reside. By 2005, approximately 7.5 billion trips were made each day in cities worldwide. In 2050, there may be three to four times as many passenger-kilometres travelled as in the year 2000, infrastructure and energy prices permitting. Freight movement could also rise more than threefold during the same period. Mobility flows have become a key dynamic of urbanization, with the associated infrastructure invariably constituting the backbone of urban form. Yet, despite the increasing level of urban mobility worldwide, access to places, activities and services has become increasingly difficult. Not only is it less convenient ― in terms of time, cost and comfort ― to access locations in cities, but the very process of moving around in cities generates a number of negative externalities. Accordingly, many of the world’s cities face an unprecedented accessibility crisis, and are characterized by unsustainable mobility systems.
다음 글의 제목으로 가장 적절한 것은?
① Is Hyper-mobility Always Good for Cities?② Accessibility: A Guide to a Web of Urban Areas③ A Long and Winding Road to Economic Success④ Inevitable Regional Conflicts from Hyper-mobility⑤ Infrastructure: An Essential Element of Hyper-mobility

24제목STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-06 · #35
Hyper-mobility ― the notion that more travel at faster speeds covering longer distances generates greater economic success ― seems to be a distinguishing feature of urban areas, where more than half of the world’s population currently reside. By 2005, approximately 7.5 billion trips were made each day in cities worldwide. In 2050, there may be three to four times as many passenger-kilometres travelled as in the year 2000, infrastructure and energy prices permitting. Freight movement could also rise more than threefold during the same period. Mobility flows have become a key dynamic of urbanization, with the associated infrastructure invariably constituting the backbone of urban form. 역접Yet, 양보despite the increasing level of urban mobility worldwide, access to places, activities and services has become increasingly difficult. 강조Not only is it less convenient ― in terms of time, cost and comfort ― to access locations in cities, but the very process of moving around in cities generates a number of negative externalities. 결론Accordingly, many of the world’s cities face an unprecedented accessibility crisis, and are characterized by unsustainable mobility systems.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①초이동성 = 더 빠르고 먼 이동이 더 큰 경제적 성공을 낳는다는 통념 — — 초이동성을 긍정적으로 보는 통념 제시
  2. 노랑②그런데도(Yet) 이동성이 늘수록 장소·서비스 접근은 오히려 더 어려워졌다 — — 역접: 통념과 반대되는 현실
  3. 노랑③따라서(Accordingly) 도시들은 유례없는 접근성 위기·지속불가능한 이동 체계에 직면 — — 결론: 초이동성이 늘 좋은 것은 아니다
→ 초이동성이 항상 도시에 좋은가에 대한 의문 제기 = ① Is Hyper-mobility Always Good for Cities?
통행량·화물 수치(2005·2050)는 예시라 skip. Yet·Accordingly 두 문장이 제목 근거.
패러프레이즈 Yet ~ access has become increasingly difficult · accessibility crisis → Is Hyper-mobility Always Good for Cities? ✓ / 복사 함정 economic success Infrastructure

24제목STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Yet, despite the increasing level of urban mobility, access to places, activities and services has become increasingly difficult.
그런데도, 도시 이동성이 증가함에도, 장소·활동·서비스에 대한 접근은 점점 더 어려워졌다. ← 역접 근거
2
Accordingly, many of the world’s cities face an unprecedented accessibility crisis, and are characterized by unsustainable mobility systems.
따라서, 세계의 많은 도시가 유례없는 접근성 위기에 직면하고, 지속 불가능한 이동 체계로 특징지어진다. ← 결론 근거
①정답 초이동성은 늘 좋은가?(Yet·접근성 위기) · ③⑤economic success·infrastructure 표면어휘 · ②④지역망·지역 갈등(근거 없음)

24제목 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Before the 1950s, tourism was very much an industry which was fragmented; hotels, transport operators, travel agents, and tour operators all tended to work independently of each other. Hotels were largely in the business of selling bed nights. Airlines and railways were in the business of selling seats. Travel agents, of course, were selling travel and holidays, but in each case they tended to operate very much as individual businesses. From the mid-1950s onwards, particularly in the UK, the growth of tour operators began to change the nature of the industry from essentially individual business activities to more integrated activities. Hotels, for example, were beginning to see customers as wanting a range of services rather than simply buying accommodation. So hotels began to develop shopping arcades and later to offer secretarial centres to try to increase the spend of guests within the hotel complex. Transport operators, particularly in the airline business, saw the sale of transport services as being integral to a much wider need. Airlines offered insurance and accommodation booking for travellers.

Transport operators, particularly in the airline business, saw the sale of transport services as being integral to a much wider need. Airlines offered insurance and accommodation booking for travellers.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①1950년대 이전 관광은 분절·서로 독립적 — — 이전 상태
  2. 노랑②1950년대 중반 이후 개별 → 통합으로 변화 — — 전환
→ 분리에서 결합(통합)으로의 변화 = ②
회색(호텔·항공 사례)은 모두 통합의 예시일 뿐.
패러프레이즈 fragmented · work independently → more integrated activities → Separate to Combined ✓
①무너지는 관광 자원의 밝고 어두운 면✘ 없음
②분리에서 결합으로: 관광업의 변화✔ 근거
③관광 산업에서 여행사 역할의 축소✘ 정반대
④통합의 실패: 고객의 다양한 요구 미충족✘ 정반대
⑤현대 관광에서 서비스 제공자의 새로운 도전✘ 없음
공식주제문을 압축·비유한 선지
24제목STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Before the 1950s, tourism was very much an industry which was fragmented; ... all tended to work independently of each other.
1950년대 이전, 관광은 매우 분절된 산업이었다; … 모두가 서로 독립적으로 일하는 경향이 있었다. ← 근거(이전 상태)
2
From the mid-1950s onwards, the growth of tour operators began to change the nature of the industry from essentially individual business activities to more integrated activities.
1950년대 중반 이후로, 여행사(투어 오퍼레이터)의 성장이 산업의 성격을 바꾸기 시작했다 본질적으로 개별적 사업 활동에서 더 통합된 활동으로. ← 근거(변화)
②정답 분리→결합, 관광업의 변화 · ①밝고 어두운 면 ③여행사 역할 축소 ④통합의 실패 ⑤새로운 도전

24제목 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Food, as we all know, is essential for human life. It also is the basis for several major industries found in many countries around the world such as in agriculture, food processing, food retailing and food service. For millennia, the focus of those involved with food as a human and economic phenomenon was on its production, preservation, distribution, pricing and other practical concerns. But in the late 18th century this began to change. Food became more than just a life necessity. Restaurants began to be developed, initially in France but eventually in other nations, as a distinct institution offering people dining choices and table service, the opportunity for socialization and, over time, a finer and finer atmosphere. The rise of restaurants eventually led to a class of diners who prided themselves on being critics of taste, food and cooking. Brillat-Savarin is probably the best known of the ‘culinary philosophers’ or, in today’s parlance, a ‘foodie’. One of Brillat-Savarin’s better known sayings was, ‘[t]ell me what you eat, and I’ll tell you who you are.’

24제목 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
The approach, joint cognitive systems, treats a robot as part of a human-machine team where the intelligence is synergistic, arising from the contributions of each agent. The team consists of at least one robot and one human and is often called a mixed team because it is a mixture of human and robot agents. Self-driving cars, where a person turns on and off the driving, is an example of a joint cognitive system. Entertainment robots are examples of mixed teams as are robots for telecommuting. The design process concentrates on how the agents will cooperate and coordinate with each other to accomplish the team goals. Rather than treating robots as peer agents with their own completely independent agenda, joint cognitive systems approaches treat robots as helpers such as service animals or sheep dogs. In joint cognitive system designs, artificial intelligence is used along with human-robot interaction principles to create robots that can be intelligent enough to be good team members.

The approach, joint cognitive systems, treats a robot as part of a human-machine team where the intelligence is 정의synergistic, arising from the contributions of each agent. The team consists of at least one robot and one human and is often called a mixed team 이유because it is a mixture of human and robot agents. 🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)

  3. 노랑③AI로 좋은 팀 구성원이 될 로봇을 만든다 — — 결론: 인간과 함께 일하는 로봇

24제목 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Monasteries were the engine rooms of the Middle Ages. At the height of their activities and influence, monasteries provided intellectual leadership for the institutions of Church and civil governments, innovation in religious thought and practice, medical provision, education, visual culture and agricultural development. They did all this while apparently observing self-imposed isolation from the wider community. For monasteries were intended to function as places set apart from the world, in which monks devoted their lives to a permanent rhythm of religious observance, prayer and study. Religious prayer and praise lay at the heart of monasticism. Both those following this life and those outside believed that monastic lives were led for the benefit of wider society, and that the sacrifices made by monks in separating themselves from ‘normal’ human contact functioned as penances on behalf of the community as well as for their own deliverance. Monks were regarded as leading parallel lives that had the power to save themselves and others.

24제목STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2026-09 · #39
Monasteries were the 핵심어engine rooms of the Middle Ages. 핵심어 At the height of their activities and influence, monasteries provided intellectual leadership for the institutions of Church and civil governments, innovation in religious thought and practice, medical provision, education, visual culture and agricultural development. 양보/대조 They did all this while apparently observing self-imposed isolation from the wider community. For monasteries were intended to function as places set apart from the world, in which monks devoted their lives to a permanent rhythm of religious observance, prayer and study. Religious prayer and praise lay at the heart of monasticism. 핵심어 Both those following this life and those outside believed that monastic lives were led for the benefit of wider society, and that the sacrifices made by monks in separating themselves from ‘normal’ human contact functioned as penances on behalf of the community as well as for their own deliverance. Monks were regarded as leading parallel lives that had the power to 결론save themselves and others.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①수도원은 중세의 지적·의료·교육·농업 리더십을 사회에 제공했다 — — 사회에 결정적으로 기여(=attached)
  2. 노랑②그러면서도 공동체로부터 스스로 고립을 지켰다 — — 세상과 분리(=detached)
  3. 노랑③수도승의 분리(고독)는 공동체를 위한 속죄로 여겨졌고, 자신과 타인을 구원 — — 고독이 곧 사회를 위한 것
→ 세상과 ‘분리’돼 있으면서도 사회에 ‘연결’된 고독의 은총 = ③
회색(‘engine rooms’ 도입·기도가 핵심이라는 부연)은 배경 설명.
패러프레이즈 self-imposed isolation + for the benefit of wider society → Detached from but Attached to Society ✓ / 복사 함정 Isolated Sacrificial Solitude Benefits

🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
They did all this while apparently observing self-imposed isolation from the wider community.
그들은 이 모든 것을 했다 겉으로는 지키면서 더 넓은 공동체로부터의 자발적 고립을. ← 근거(분리 detached)
2
the sacrifices made by monks in separating themselves functioned as penances on behalf of the community as well as for their own deliverance.
수도승들이 자신을 분리함으로써 한 희생은 공동체를 대신한 속죄로 기능했다 자신의 구원뿐 아니라. ← 근거(연결 attached)
③정답 고독의 은총: 분리돼 있으나 사회에 연결됨 · ①혜택 얻는 법 ②고립 강요 이유 ④희생적 지도자론 ⑤권력·정치

24제목 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Not only musicians and psychologists, but also committed music enthusiasts and experts often voice the opinion that the beauty of music lies in an expressive deviation from the exactly defined score. Concert performances become interesting and gain in attraction from the fact that they go far beyond the information printed in the score. In his early studies on musical performance, Carl Seashore discovered that musicians only rarely play two equal notes in exactly the same way. Within the same metric structure, there is a wide potential of variations in tempo, volume, tonal quality and intonation. Such variation is based on the composition but diverges from it individually. We generally call this ‘expressivity’. This explains why we do not lose interest when we hear different artists perform the same piece of music. It also explains why it is worthwhile for following generations to repeat the same repertoire. New, inspiring interpretations help us to expand our understanding, which serves to enrich and animate the music scene.
다음 글의 제목으로 가장 적절한 것은?

24제목STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-09 · #40 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
강조Not only musicians and psychologists, but also committed music enthusiasts and experts often voice the opinion that the beauty of music lies in an expressive deviation from the exactly defined score. Concert performances become interesting and gain in attraction from the fact that they go far beyond the information printed in the score. In his early studies on musical performance, Carl Seashore discovered that musicians only rarely play two equal notes in exactly the same way. Within the same metric structure, there is a wide potential of variations in tempo, volume, tonal quality and intonation. Such variation is based on the composition 대조but diverges from it individually. We generally call this ‘expressivity’. This explains why we do not lose interest when we hear different artists perform the same piece of music. It also explains why it is worthwhile for following generations to repeat the same repertoire. New, inspiring interpretations help us to expand our understanding, which serves to enrich and animate the music scene.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①음악의 아름다움은 악보에서의 표현적 일탈(변주)에 있다 — — 핵심어: 변주·표현성
  2. 노랑②이 변주를 ‘표현성(expressivity)’이라 부른다 — — 개념 명명
  3. 노랑③그래서 연주자마다 달라도 흥미를 잃지 않는다 — — 변주의 가치 = 지루하지 않음
→ 이으면: 연주에서 변주(달라짐)의 가치 = ② (Never the Same)
Seashore 연구·tempo/volume 나열은 예시(회색), 빼도 무방.

24제목 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Mending and restoring objects often require even more creativity than original production. The preindustrial blacksmith made things to order for people in his immediate community; customizing the product, modifying or transforming it according to the user, was routine. Customers would bring things back if something went wrong; repair was thus an extension of fabrication. With industrialization and eventually with mass production, making things became the province of machine tenders with limited knowledge. But repair continued to require a larger grasp of design and materials, an understanding of the whole and a comprehension of the designer's intentions. “Manufacturers all work by machinery or by vast subdivision of labour and not, so to speak, by hand,” an 1896 Manual of Mending and Repairing explained. “But all repairing must be done by hand. We can make every detail of a watch or of a gun by machinery, but the machine cannot mend it when broken, much less a clock or a pistol!”

24제목STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2022-수능 · #41 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
Mending and restoring objects often require even 주제more creativity than original production. The preindustrial blacksmith made things to order for people in his immediate community; customizing the product, modifying or transforming it according to the user, was routine. Customers would bring things back if something went wrong; repair was thus an extension of fabrication. 대조With industrialization and eventually with mass production, making things became the province of machine tenders with limited knowledge. 역접But repair 지속continued to require a larger grasp of design and materials, an understanding of the whole and a comprehension of the designer's intentions. “Manufacturers all work by machinery or by vast subdivision of labour and not, so to speak, by hand,” an 1896 Manual of Mending and Repairing explained. “역접But all repairing 당위must be done by hand. We can make every detail of a watch or of a gun by machinery, 역접but the machine cannot mend it when broken, much less a clock or a pistol!”
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①수리·복원은 원래 제작보다 '더 큰 창의력'을 요구한다 — — 주제문
  2. 노랑②산업화로 '제작'은 기계공의 일이 됐지만 '수리'는 여전히 설계·전체 이해를 요구한다 — — But continued, 수리는 예외
  3. 노랑③'모든 수리는 손으로 해야 한다(must be done by hand)'—기계는 못 고친다 — — 수리는 여전히 사람의 영역
→ 수리(장인의 손기술)는 산업화 후에도 남은 '기술=예술' = ①
blacksmith 주문제작 예시는 부연—핵심은 '수리는 손·창의' 대비.

24제목STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Mending and restoring objects often require even more creativity than original production.
물건을 고치고 복원하는 것은 흔히 더 큰 창의력을 요구한다 원래의 제작보다. ← 근거
2
But repair continued to require a larger grasp of design and materials, a comprehension of the designer's intentions.
하지만 수리는 여전히 요구했다 설계와 재료에 대한 더 큰 파악, 설계자 의도에 대한 이해를. ← 근거
3
But all repairing must be done by hand.
하지만 모든 수리는 손으로 이루어져야 한다. ← 근거
①정답 현대 대장장이에게 남겨진 것: 수리라는 예술 · ②수리 기술 진화 역사(단순 개관) ③수리 팁 안내 ④제작·변형 과정 ⑤산업화가 과거를 고칠까(무관)
wbspread
24제목 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
As far back as 32,000 years ago, prehistoric cave artists skillfully used modeling shadows to give their horses and bison volume. A few thousand years ago ancient Egyptian and then ancient Greek art presented human forms in shadow-style silhouette. But cast shadows do not appear in Western art until about 400 BCE in Athens. It was only after shadows had become an established, if controversial, part of representation that classical writers claimed that art itself had begun with the tracing of a human shadow. Greeks and Romans were the first to make the transition from modeling shadows to cast shadows, a practice that implied a consistent light source, a fixed point of view, and an understanding of geometric projection. In fact, what we might now call “shadow studies” ― the exploration of shadows in their various artistic representations ― has its roots in ancient Athens. Ever since, the practice of portraying shadows has evolved along with critical analysis of them, as artists and theoreticians have engaged in an ongoing debate about the significance of shadow representation.

🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①3만 2천 년 전 선사 동굴 화가가 '모델링 그림자'로 말·들소에 입체감을 줌 — — 미술 속 그림자의 출발점
  2. 노랑②그리스·로마가 모델링→투영 그림자로 전환한 최초 (일관된 광원·시점·기하 투영 함의) — — 역사적 발전 단계
  3. 노랑③그 이후로(Ever since) 그림자 묘사가 비평적 분석과 함께 계속 진화 — — 현재까지 이어지는 여정
→ 선사 동굴부터 이후로 이어진 미술 속 그림자의 '여정(역사)' = ①

멀게는 3만 2천 년 전에, 선사시대 동굴 화가들은 모델링 그림자를 능숙하게 사용했다 말과 들소에 입체감을 주기 위해. ← 기원(출발점)

그 이후로, 그림자를 묘사하는 관행은 진화해 왔다 그것에 대한 비평적 분석과 함께, 예술가와 이론가가 지속적 논쟁을 벌이면서. ← 근거(현재까지 이어짐)

24제목 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
There are good reasons why open-office plans have gained currency, but open offices may not be the plan of choice for all times. Instead, the right plan seems to be building a culture of change. Overly rigid habits and conventions, no matter how well-considered or well-intentioned, threaten innovation. The crucial take-away from analyzing office plans over time is that the answers keep changing. It might seem that there is a straight line of progress, but it’s a myth. Surveying office spaces from the past eighty years, one can see a cycle that repeats. Comparing the offices of the 1940s with contemporary office spaces shows that they have circled back around to essentially the same style, via a period in the 1980s when partitions and cubicles were more the norm. The technologies and colors may differ, but the 1940s and 2000s plans are alike, right down to the pillars running down the middle.

The 결론crucial take-away from analyzing office plans over time is that the answers keep changing. It might seem that there is a straight line of progress, 역접but it’s a myth. Surveying office spaces from the past eighty years, one can see a 핵심cycle that repeats. Comparing the offices of the 1940s with contemporary office spaces shows that they have 예시circled back around to essentially the same style, via a period in the 1980s when partitions and cubicles were more the norm. The technologies and colors may differ, but the 1940s and 2000s plans are alike, right down to the pillars running down the middle.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①직선적 진보는 착각(myth)이다 — — 발전이 한 방향으로만 간다는 통념을 부정
  2. 노랑②지난 80년을 보면 반복되는 순환이 보인다 — — 스타일이 돌고 돈다는 핵심
  3. 노랑③1940년대와 현대가 같은 스타일로 회귀 — — 순환의 구체적 증거
→ 사무실 디자인은 직선 진보가 아니라 돌고 돈다 = ④
개방형 사무실 도입 이유(도입부)는 소재일 뿐, 논리에 필수 아님.

Writers often give us the impression that they have described the faces of their characters, when in fact they have simply given you an outline to fill in. Of Esch, the most important character in Hermann Broch’s masterpiece The Sleepwalkers, we learn only that he has big teeth. Even so, we don’t feel as if his face is a dentate blankness. Most often, we mistake being told what effect someone’s appearance has for an account of that appearance. The poet Mallarmé’s advice ― Peindre non la chose, mais l’effet qu’elle produit (‘Paint not the thing itself but the effect it produces’) sounds like a self-denying ordinance. Actually it is a rather clever way out of an intractable problem. When, in one of his novels, Evelyn Waugh says of a new character, that ‘he had just the kind of appearance one would expect a young man of his type to have’ and nothing else, you still feel as if you

The dependent and shifting nature of art values, not only on the market but inherently, is uppermost in the mind of today’s art collector, whether he buys for himself or as the representative of an institution. Acquiring a work is acquiring a piece of art history, or it is acquiring nothing (beyond, that is, an object of personal enjoyment in the same class as a cat or a souvenir). In signing his check the collector asserts his belief in the future presence of the work as a significant point attained by art as a whole. History, however, is open to anything and the merit of the chosen painting or sculpture has ―at best ―only the validation of a present-day consensus. This consensus is all but certain to be displaced, as others have been in the past. In the last analysis, committing himself to a painting is the collector’s own act. Through it he courageously affirms not only his aesthetic judgment, from whatever source it be derived, but the conviction

People have always wanted to be around other people and to learn from them. Cities have long been dynamos of social possibility, foundries of art, music, and fashion. Slang, or, if you prefer, “lexical innovation,” has always started in cities ― an outgrowth of all those different people so frequently exposed to one another. It spreads outward, in a manner not unlike transmissible disease, which itself typically “takes off” in cities. If, as the noted linguist Leonard Bloomfield argued, the way a person talks is a “composite result of what he has heard before,” then language innovation would happen where the most people heard and talked to the most other people. Cities drive taste change because they    , who not surprisingly are often the creative people cities seem to attract. Media, ever more global, ever more far-reaching, spread language faster to more people.

32빈칸추론STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-수능 · #46 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
People have always wanted to be around other people and to learn from them. Cities have long been dynamos of social possibility, foundries of art, music, and fashion. Slang, or, if you prefer, “lexical innovation,” has always started in cities ― an outgrowth of all those different people so frequently 핵심exposed to one another. It spreads outward, in a manner not unlike transmissible disease, which itself typically “takes off” in cities. 조건If, as the noted linguist Leonard Bloomfield argued, the way a person talks is a “composite result of what he has heard before,” then language innovation would happen where the most people heard and talked to the most other people. Cities drive taste change 인과because they ______, who not surprisingly are often the creative people cities seem to attract. Media, ever more global, ever more far-reaching, spread language faster to more people.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①속어·언어 혁신은 서로 노출된 다양한 사람들에게서 시작된다 — — 핵심은 '많은 사람과의 노출·접촉'
  2. 노랑②언어 혁신은 가장 많은 사람이 서로 듣고 말하는 곳에서 일어난다 — — 도시가 바로 그 조건을 충족
→ 도시가 유행을 이끄는 이유 = 사람들에게 '가장 많은 노출'을 제공하기 때문 = ②
회색(도입·미디어 부연)은 빼도 '노출 → 혁신' 논리에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 exposed to one another · heard and talked to the most other people → offer the greatest exposure to other people ✓ / 복사 함정 artists linguists
①provide rich source materials for artists✘ 표면어휘
②offer the greatest exposure to other people✔ 근거
③cause cultural conflicts among users of slang✘ 없음
④present ideal research environments to linguists✘ 표면어휘
⑤reduce the social mobility of ambitious outsiders✘ 없음
공식역접·한정 문장 추상화 / 지문어 복사 선지 = 함정

Slang has always started in cities ― an outgrowth of all those different people so frequently exposed to one another.

33빈칸추론 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
When we narrow, we’re redirecting all of our computing power to the handful of processes that matter. It’s as if to help with our slow Wi-Fi, we disconnect our phone and tablet, just so that our video conference call won’t lag. Narrowing also helps with goal attainment. It cuts out all of the other distractions and places the most important goal front and center. When we home in, we increase motivational intensity, reinforcing that what’s in front of us is what we should be after. For a brief moment, the trade-off can be worthwhile, but when we    , we start to miss cues and signals. We get locked in on one path without being able to step back and see a better route. When we’re stuck narrowed in for too long, accidents go up and performance drops. We miss hearing alarms that signal there’s a problem elsewhere.
빈칸에 들어갈 말로 가장 적절한 것은?
① get distracted by too many sources② rely too heavily on digital devices③ randomly switch between tasks④ remain zoomed in for too long⑤ fail to control our feelings

34빈칸추론 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
One of the common themes of the Western philosophical tradition is the distinction between sensual perceptions and rational knowledge. Since Plato, the supremacy of rational reason is based on the assertion that it is able to extract true knowledge from experience. As the discussion in the Republic helps to explain, perceptions are inherently unreliable and misleading because the senses are subject to errors and illusions. Only the rational discourse has the tools to overcome illusions and to point towards true knowledge. For instance, perception suggests that a figure in the distance is smaller than it really is. Yet, the application of logical reasoning will reveal that the figure only appears small because it obeys the laws of geometrical perspective. Nevertheless, even after the perspectival correction is applied and reason concludes that perception is misleading, the figure still appears small, and the truth of the matter is revealed
빈칸에 들어갈 말로 가장 적절한 것은?
① as the outcome of blindly following sensual experience② by moving away from the idea of perfect representation③ beyond the limit of where rational knowledge can approach④ through a variety of experiences rather than logical reasoning⑤ not in the perception of the figure but in its rational representation

34빈칸추론STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-06 · #48
One of the common themes of the Western philosophical tradition is the distinction between sensual perceptions and rational knowledge. Since Plato, the supremacy of rational reason is based on the assertion that it is able to extract true knowledge from experience. As the discussion in the Republic helps to explain, perceptions are inherently unreliable and misleading 이유because the senses are subject to errors and illusions. 한정Only the rational discourse has the tools to overcome illusions and to point towards true knowledge. For instance, perception suggests that a figure in the distance is smaller than it really is. 역접Yet, the application of logical reasoning will reveal that the figure only appears small because it obeys the laws of geometrical perspective. 역접Nevertheless, even after the perspectival correction is applied and reason concludes that perception is misleading, the figure still appears small, and the truth of the matter is revealed
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①감각적 지각은 본래 믿을 수 없고 오해를 부른다 — — 지각=오류의 원천
  2. 노랑②오직 이성적 담론만이 환상을 극복하고 참된 지식을 가리킨다 — — Only: 진리는 이성에서
  3. 노랑③지각이 오해임을 알아도 형체는 여전히 작아 보이므로, 진리는 지각이 아니라 이성적 표상에서 드러난다 — — Nevertheless: 진리의 소재 = 이성
→ 진리는 형체의 지각이 아니라 그 이성적 표상에서 드러난다 = ⑤
도입 정의·원근법 예시(For instance/Yet)는 뒷받침일 뿐, 핵심은 '오직 이성'.
패러프레이즈 Only rational discourse points towards true knowledge → in its rational representation ✓ / 복사 함정 sensual experience logical reasoning representation
⑤not in the perception of the figure but in its rational representation✔ 근거
①as the outcome of blindly following sensual experience✘ 정반대
②by moving away from the idea of perfect representation✘ 없음
③beyond the limit of where rational knowledge can approach✘ 정반대
④through a variety of experiences rather than logical reasoning✘ 정반대
공식역접·한정 문장 추상화 / 지문어 복사 선지 = 함정

오직 이성적 담론만이 환상을 극복할 도구를 가지며 참된 지식을 가리킨다. ← 근거

33빈칸추론 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Compared to other ecosystems, forests are relatively diverse, but this should not necessarily be a justification for converting nonforests into forests. Wetlands, meadows, and grasslands have a unique biota too, even if it is often not as rich as a forest biota. The ecological problems of this process have been described from a number of places such as Iceland, South Africa, and Australia, but the classic example of this comes from Scotland and northern England. Here the Forestry Commission has drained, fertilized, and fenced extensive areas of wetlands to facilitate turning them into forests. Increasing the extent of forests in Britain is certainly a desirable goal, and most of the Forestry Commission’s efforts are directed toward sites that were forested before sheep and their keepers came to the island. However, ecologists frequently complain about the Commission’s work because it is not restricted to former forest sites, because the forests established are usually composed of exotic trees, and because the wildlife threatened by this activity includes many uncommon species.
빈칸에 들어갈 말로 가장 적절한 것은?
① grounds for investigating the value of forest expansion② a basis for extending institutional aid to entire ecosystems③ taken as the reason for introducing exotic species to an ecosystem④ accepted as evidence for increasing ecological enrichment⑤ a justification for converting nonforests into forests

  1. 노랑①숲이 상대적으로 다양하다, 그러나 이것이 ___이 되어선 안 된다 — — but 뒤 빈칸: 숲의 다양성을 근거로 삼는 일
  2. 노랑②습지·초원도 고유한 생물군을 가진다 — — 비(非)숲도 나름의 가치가 있다
  3. 노랑③However 생태학자들은 습지를 숲으로 바꾸는 일을 비판 — — 결국 비숲을 숲으로 '전환'하는 정당화가 문제
→ 세 문장을 이으면: 숲의 다양성이 비숲을 숲으로 바꾸는 정당화가 될 순 없다 = ⑤
회색(스코틀랜드 사례·산림위원회 조치)은 빈칸 논리를 예시로 뒷받침할 뿐.
패러프레이즈 forests diverse but ... / Wetlands have unique biota / ecologists complain → a justification for converting nonforests into forests ✓ / 복사 함정 exotic species expansion

그러나 생태학자들은 자주 불평한다 원래 숲이던 곳에 한정되지 않기에, 위협받는 야생생물에 희귀종이 포함되기에 ← 근거
①숲 확장 가치 조사 근거 ②전 생태계 지원 근거 ③외래종 도입 이유(표면어휘) ④생태적 풍부화 증거(정반대) · ⑤정답 비숲을 숲으로 전환하는 정당화

40빈칸추론 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Even those with average talent can produce notable work in the various sciences, so long as they do not try to embrace all of them at once. Instead, they should concentrate attention on one subject after another (that is, in different periods of time), although later work will weaken earlier attainments in the other spheres. This amounts to saying that the brain adapts to universal science in time but not in space. In fact, even those with great abilities proceed in this way. Thus, when we are astonished by someone with publications in different scientific fields, realize that each topic was explored during a specific period of time. Knowledge gained earlier certainly will not have disappeared from the mind of the author, but it will have become simplified by condensing into formulas or greatly abbreviated symbols. Thus, sufficient space remains for the perception and learning of new images on the cerebral blackboard. Exploring one scientific subject after another remarkable work across the sciences, as the previously gained knowledge is retained in simplified forms within the brain, which room for new learning.

40빈칸추론STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #50
Even those with average talent can produce notable work in the various sciences, 조건so long as they do not try to embrace all of them at once. 대조Instead, they should concentrate attention on one subject after another (that is, in different periods of time), although later work will weaken earlier attainments in the other spheres. This amounts to saying that the brain adapts to universal science in time 한정but not in space. In fact, even those with great abilities proceed in this way. 결론Thus, when we are astonished by someone with publications in different scientific fields, realize that each topic was explored during a specific period of time. Knowledge gained earlier certainly will not have disappeared from the mind of the author, 역접but it will have become simplified by condensing into formulas or greatly abbreviated symbols. 결론Thus, sufficient space remains for the perception and learning of new images on the cerebral blackboard. Exploring one scientific subject after another remarkable work across the sciences, as the previously gained knowledge is retained in simplified forms within the brain, which room for new learning.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①한 주제씩 차례로 집중하면 뛰어난 성과가 나온다 — — 여러 과학 분야에서 주목할 성과를 '만들어낸다(=긍정 결과)'
  2. 노랑②먼저 얻은 지식은 사라지지 않고 공식·기호로 단순화되어 저장된다 — — 지식이 '단순화된 형태로 유지(retained)'된다
  3. 노랑③그래서 새 학습을 위한 충분한 공간이 남는다 — — 뇌에 '여지를 남긴다(leaves room)'
→ 세 문장을 이으면: 한 주제씩 탐구함이 성과를 '낳고(A)', 단순화 저장이 새 학습 공간을 '남긴다(B)' = ①
회색(도입 문장·놀라움 예시)은 빼도 논리에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 sufficient space remains·retained in simplified forms → leaves room ✓ / 복사 함정 challenges limits
①produces … leaves✔ 근거
②produces … limits✘ 정반대
③delays … leaves✘ 정반대
④challenges … limits✘ 정반대
⑤enables … occupies✘ 정반대
공식역접·한정 문장 추상화 / 지문어 복사 선지 = 함정

The human psychology and education communities (as well as some animal researchers!) have been against referring to “teaching” when describing social learning in animals, mainly because teaching implies a level of     on the part of the model that is difficult to measure in animals. Nonetheless, there has been a movement within the animal cognition community to say that animals can, in fact, teach one another. For example, skilled ants engage in a behavior called tandem running, in which they touch their bodies to the body of a novice ant as they lay down chemical trails, presumably to assist the newcomer with route learning. Killer whales also repeat the same seal hunting technique in front of their offspring, sometimes without even killing the seal, leading researchers to ask why they would repeatedly catch and release a seal if they were not planning to eat it. Considering the amount of energy they’d have to expend, there would need to be a good reason, and that reason might be teaching.

34빈칸추론 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
When gathering the preferences of multiple agents into one collective choice, it is easily seen that certain cases    . For example, if there are two alternatives, a and b, and two agents such that one prefers a and the other one b, there is no deterministic way of selecting a single alternative without violating one of two basic fairness conditions known as anonymity and neutrality. Anonymity requires that the collective choice ought to be independent of the agents' identities whereas neutrality requires impartiality towards the alternatives. Allowing lotteries as social outcomes hence seems like a necessity for impartial collective choice. Indeed, most common “deterministic” social choice functions such as plurality rule are only deterministic as long as there is no tie, which is usually resolved by drawing a lot. The use of lotteries for the selection of officials interestingly goes back to the world's first democracy in Athens, where it was widely regarded as a principal characteristic of democracy, and has recently gained increasing attention in political science.
빈칸에 들어갈 말로 가장 적절한 것은?
① call for randomization or other means of tiebreaking② demonstrate how impartial selection could be invalidated③ necessitate deterministic systems of selecting voters blindly④ rely upon neutral agents to establish clear selection guidelines⑤ require understanding of the unpredictability inherent in democracy

31빈칸추론 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Ever since the early Enlightenment, preservation and conservation have been closely related. Taken as near synonyms, their meaning is to maintain an object or system insofar as possible in its present state, to protect it from change, usually for contemplation, research, display, and perhaps for use. Conservationists who distinguish their activities from preservation emphasize conservation’s restorative aspects ― restoring a historical musical instrument, for example, or a painting, or a dinosaur, or an ecosystem. Conservationists acknowledge change but try to manage it in order to prolong     state. Preservationists (who may nonetheless call themselves conservationists) think of themselves more as protectors. They sometimes criticize conservationists for setting an additional priority on yield or harvest or use, rather than interfering as minimally as possible in order to preserve the original object or system, as they would do. Preservationists would, for example, prefer to keep a historical musical instrument “as found” in a deteriorated state, for study, rather than to restore or repair it for display or use.

① desired② unstable③ isolated④ natural⑤ delicate

31빈칸추론STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2027-06 · #53
Ever since the early Enlightenment, preservation and conservation have been closely related. Taken as near synonyms, their meaning is to maintain an object or system insofar as possible in its present state, to protect it from change, usually for contemplation, research, display, and perhaps for use. Conservationists who 대조distinguish their activities from preservation emphasize conservation’s restorative aspects ― restoring a historical musical instrument, for example, or a painting, or a dinosaur, or an ecosystem. Conservationists acknowledge change 역접but try to manage it in order to prolong ______ state. Preservationists (who may 양보nonetheless call themselves conservationists) think of themselves more as protectors. They sometimes criticize conservationists for setting an additional priority on yield or harvest or use, 대조rather than interfering as minimally as possible in order to preserve the original object or system, as they would do. Preservationists would, for example, prefer to keep a historical musical instrument “as found” in a deteriorated state, for study, rather than to restore or repair it for display or use.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①보존가(conservationist)는 복원(restorative)을 강조하고 변화를 ‘관리’한다 — — 원래 상태 그대로가 아니라 원하는 방향으로 바꿔간다
  2. 노랑②그들은 산출·수확·사용(yield/harvest/use)에 추가 우선순위를 둔다(개입 최소화하는 보존론자와 대조) — — 유용하고 원하는 결과를 위해 변화를 조절한다
→ prolong ______ state = 관리해 도달하려는 ‘원하는’ 상태 = ① desired
도입·정의·예시(회색)는 빼도 논리에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 manage change·priority on yield/harvest/use·restorative → desired ✓
①desired (원하는)✔ 근거
②unstable (불안정한)✘ 정반대
③isolated (고립된)✘ 없음
④natural (자연 그대로의)✘ 정반대
⑤delicate (섬세한)✘ 없음

A musical score within any film can add an additional layer to the film text, which goes beyond simply imitating the action viewed. In films that tell of futuristic worlds, composers, much like sound designers, have added freedom to create a world that is unknown and new to the viewer. However, unlike sound designers, composers often shy away from creating unique pieces that reflect these new worlds and often present musical scores that possess familiar structures and cadences. While it is possible that this may interfere with creativity and a sense of space and time, it in fact     Through recognizable scores, visions of the future or a galaxy far, far away can be placed within a recognizable context. Such familiarity allows the viewer to be placed in a comfortable space so that the film may then lead the viewer to what is an unfamiliar, but acceptable vision of a world different from their

A musical score within any film can add an additional layer to the film text, which goes beyond simply imitating the action viewed. In films that tell of futuristic worlds, composers, much like sound designers, have added freedom to create a world that is unknown and new to the viewer. 역접However, unlike sound designers, composers often shy away from creating unique pieces that reflect these new worlds and often present musical scores that possess familiar structures and cadences. 양보While it is possible that this may interfere with creativity and a sense of space and time, it 강조in fact ___ Through recognizable scores, visions of the future or a galaxy far, far away can be placed within a 핵심recognizable context. 🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①작곡가는 익숙한 구조의 음악을 쓴다 — — 낯선 세계에도 친숙한 선율
  2. 노랑②친숙한 악보로 미래·먼 은하를 익숙한 맥락에 놓는다 — — 낯선 것을 알아볼 수 있게 만듦
  3. 노랑③관객을 편안한 공간에 두어 영화로 이끈다 — — 관객의 진입을 도움
→ 익숙한 음악은 창의성을 해치기는커녕 관객의 영화 접근을 돕는다 = ②
도입부(작곡가의 자유)는 대비용 배경, 정답 논리엔 불필요.
패러프레이즈 comfortable space·recognizable context·lead the viewer → aids in viewer access ✓ / 복사 함정 familiarity
②aids in viewer access to the film✔ 근거
①frees the plot of its familiarity✘ 정반대
③adds to an exotic musical experience✘ 정반대
