35무관한 문장 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Plants have a remarkable ability to recover from disaster. This is largely because they have preferential access to the tissues that rebuild their organs. Throughout their life cycle, plants retain meristems, which are regions of undifferentiated tissue that can differentiate into new shoots and other organs. This phenomenon occurs at very different scales, ranging from the healing of a small scar to the regrowth of an entire plant. Animals, by contrast, invest heavily in learned behaviors that help them avoid predators and find food. After being disturbed, a plant community can adjust its composition through regeneration, mainly resprouting and reseeding.

35무관한 문장STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-09 · #55 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
Plants have a remarkable ability to recover from disaster. This is largely because they have preferential access to the tissues that rebuild their organs. Throughout their life cycle, plants retain meristems, which are regions of undifferentiated tissue that can differentiate into new shoots and other organs. This phenomenon occurs at very different scales, ranging from the healing of a small scar to the regrowth of an entire plant. Animals, by contrast, invest heavily in learned behaviors that help them avoid predators and find food. After being disturbed, a plant community can adjust its composition through regeneration, mainly resprouting and reseeding.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①글의 소재는 식물이 재해에서 회복하는 능력 — — 도입: 주제 대상은 '식물'로 고정
  2. 노랑②식물은 분열조직(meristem)으로 새 기관을 재생 → 규모별로 일어남 — — 재생 메커니즘 설명, 계속 식물 얘기
  3. 노랑③④는 갑자기 동물의 학습 행동으로 소재가 이탈 — — by contrast로 대상이 '동물'로 바뀜 = 흐름 밖
→ 식물 재생이라는 한 줄기에서 벗어난 문장은 동물을 다룬 ④
규모·흉터 예시(회색·skip)는 부연일 뿐 소재는 여전히 식물.
패러프레이즈 plant regeneration·meristem → off-topic animal behavior ✓ / 복사 함정 Animals predators
①preferential access — 분열조직으로 기관 재생✘ 없음
②meristem이 새 shoot·organ으로 분화✘ 없음
③흉터 치유~식물 전체 재생, 다양한 scale✘ 없음
④Animals 학습 행동으로 포식자 회피✔ 근거
⑤교란 후 resprouting·reseeding으로 군집 재구성✘ 없음
공식주제에서 벗어난 소재·논리의 문장 1개
35무관한 문장STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Throughout their life cycle, plants retain meristems, which can differentiate into new shoots and organs.
생애 주기 내내, 식물은 분열조직을 유지하는데, 그것은 새 싹과 기관으로 분화할 수 있다. ← 근거(식물 재생 메커니즘)
2
Animals, by contrast, invest in learned behaviors to avoid predators and find food.
반면에 동물은, 학습된 행동에 투자한다 포식자를 피하고 먹이를 찾기 위해. ← 소재 이탈(=정답 ④)
④정답 동물의 학습 행동(=소재 이탈) · ①②③⑤ 모두 식물 재생 흐름 유지

35무관한 문장 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Even though organizations are offering flexible workplace arrangements such as telecommuting in ever-increasing numbers, acceptance and use of these programs are still limited by a variety of factors. These factors include manager reliance on face-to-face management practices, lack of telecommuting training within an organization, misperceptions of and discomfort with flexible workplace programs, and a limited understanding of their implications for an organization’s bottom line. One dominant barrier is the belief that physical presence is essential to genuine productivity. Because of this, employees who telecommute are often perceived as less committed than those in the office. New digital devices emerge every year, and firms compete fiercely to design products best suited for global consumer markets. With regard to such misperceptions, organizations must train managers to evaluate workers by results rather than by hours seen at a desk. Only when these barriers are removed will flexible workplace programs gain full acceptance.

③ 은 전체 글의 흐름과 관계가 없다.

🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①재택근무 제도가 늘어도 수용·활용은 여러 요인으로 제한됨(주제) — — 글의 중심 소재 = 유연근무 '수용의 장벽'
  2. 노랑②①②④⑤: 지배적 통념·오해·성과중심 교육·장벽 제거 → 모두 '수용' 논의 — — 주제선(수용 저해와 해법)으로 일관
  3. 노랑③③: 디지털 신제품이 세계 소비시장용으로 경쟁 — — 유연근무 수용과 무관한 '제품 경쟁' 소재
→ 주제(재택근무 수용)에서 벗어난 문장 = ③
정답 미제공 → 직접 풀이(solved). 글은 '유연근무 수용의 장벽·해법'이 주제이며 ③만 제품 시장 경쟁으로 이탈. 입력에 본문이 없어 어휘(수용·오해·불편·장벽·지배적·영향·~에 관련) 기반으로 재구성한 지문임.
패러프레이즈 주제 = 재택근무 도입 저해 요인·오해·해법 → ③ 신제품·세계 소비시장 경쟁(주제 이탈) ✓
③신제품이 매년 등장·세계 소비시장용 제품 경쟁✔ 무관(주제이탈)
①물리적 재실이 생산성에 필수라는 지배적 통념(장벽)✘ 흐름연결
②그래서 재택근무자는 덜 헌신적으로 오인됨✘ 흐름연결
④그런 오해와 관련해 성과 중심 평가 교육 필요✘ 흐름연결
⑤장벽이 제거돼야 유연근무가 온전히 수용됨✘ 흐름연결

Even though organizations are offering flexible workplace arrangements such as telecommuting in ever-increasing numbers, acceptance and use of these programs are still limited by a variety of factors.

③정답 신제품·세계 소비시장 경쟁(주제 이탈) · ①②④⑤재택근무 수용을 막는 요인·오해·해법으로 자연스럽게 연결

35무관한 문장 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Young songbirds do not inherit their songs fully formed; instead, they must learn to reproduce the song of an adult tutor. Early in life, a young bird memorizes this model and stores it as a kind of template against which its own attempts are later compared. At first its babbling is uneven, with the pitch and tempo of each note varying widely from one attempt to the next. The colorful feathers of many male songbirds evolved mainly to attract mates rather than to improve their singing. Through constant practice, the bird gradually fine-tunes its vocalizations, adjusting each note until it more closely matches the stored template. Eventually the young bird’s song overlaps almost completely with the tutor’s, an accurate copy produced through this long process of listening and self-correction.
다음 글에서 전체 흐름과 관계 없는 문장은?
④ 는 글의 전체 흐름과 관계가 없다.
🖍 이렇게 풀어요
1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지
📢 신호 리마인더
주제 키워드역접 However예시 For instance ✕

  • 예시·양보절은 회색으로 넘겼나?
  • 지문 단어 그대로 쓴 선지부터 지웠나?

35무관한 문장STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2025-06 · #57 (정답 미공개 → 풀이로 확정)
Young songbirds do not inherit their songs fully formed; instead, they must learn to 핵심reproduce the song of an adult tutor. Early in life, a young bird memorizes this model and stores it as a kind of 핵심template against which its own attempts are later compared. 대조At first its babbling is uneven, with the pitch and tempo of each note varying widely from one attempt to the next. The colorful feathers of many male songbirds evolved mainly to attract mates rather than to improve their singing. Through constant practice, the bird gradually 핵심fine-tunes its vocalizations, adjusting each note until it more closely matches the stored template. 결론Eventually the young bird’s song overlaps almost completely with the tutor’s, an accurate copy produced through this long process of listening and self-correction.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①어린 새는 어른 새의 노래를 본(template)으로 저장하고 이를 재현하려 한다 — — 글 전체 소재 = '노래 학습(재현)'
  2. 노랑②연습으로 각 음을 미세 조정해 저장된 본에 가깝게 맞춰 간다 — — 학습 과정의 핵심 흐름
→ ④는 '깃털이 짝을 유혹하려 진화했다'는 외형·번식 이야기 = 노래 학습 흐름과 무관 = ④
①②③⑤는 '본 저장 → 재현 → 미세조정'으로 이어지지만 ④만 소재가 벗어남.
패러프레이즈 노래 학습(재현·본·미세조정) → ④ 깃털·짝짓기(외형·번식) ✓
①본을 재현하려 학습(도입)✘ 주제부합
②본으로 저장·비교✘ 주제부합
③처음엔 음정·박자가 고르지 않음✘ 주제부합
④깃털이 짝 유혹 위해 진화✔ 근거
⑤미세 조정으로 본에 근접✘ 주제부합
공식주제에서 벗어난 소재·논리의 문장 1개
35무관한 문장STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
a young bird memorizes this model and stores it as a kind of template, against which its attempts are compared.
어린 새는 이 모델을 기억하고 그것을 일종의 본으로 저장해, 자기 시도를 그것에 견주어 비교한다. ← 근거
2
the bird gradually fine-tunes its vocalizations, adjusting each note until it matches the stored template.
새는 점차 자기 발성을 미세 조정하며, 각 음을 다듬어 저장된 본에 맞을 때까지 이른다. ← 근거
④정답 깃털이 짝 유혹 위해 진화 = 노래 학습과 무관 · ①재현 도입 ②본 저장 ③초기 불균일 ⑤미세조정
wbspread
35무관한 문장 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Most antiquarian and second-hand booksellers do not keep large stocks of any specific title, since each book they handle is usually unique. Because customers cannot simply come in and find what they want on the shelves, dealers inevitably build up their collection through other means. As a matter of course, dealers routinely circulate catalogues and lists to their regular customers, who then enquire about the availability of particular books. Full-time booksellers must, crucially, keep their shop premises open long hours so that passing tourists can browse the latest bestsellers. In this way, the reach of a specialist dealer can multiply and expand far beyond the walls of any single shop.

④ 는 글의 전체

Most antiquarian and second-hand booksellers 주제do not keep large stocks of any specific title, since each book they handle is usually unique. 인과Because customers cannot simply come in and find what they want on the shelves, dealers inevitably build up their collection through other means. As a matter of course, dealers 부연routinely circulate catalogues and lists to their regular customers, who then enquire about the availability of particular books. Full-time booksellers must, crucially, keep their shop premises open long hours so that passing tourists can browse the latest bestsellers. 결론In this way, the reach of a specialist dealer can multiply and expand far beyond the walls of any single shop.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주제: 전문 서적상은 재고를 두지 않고 카탈로그로 유통 — — 글 전체 흐름
  2. 노랑②④는 매장을 오래 열어 관광객이 베스트셀러를 봄 — — 소재·논리 이탈
→ 주제(재고 없음·카탈로그 유통)와 어긋나는 문장 = ④
①②③⑤는 모두 '재고 없이 목록으로 도달' 주제로 이어짐.
패러프레이즈 stock · circulate catalogues · availability · multiply · expand → ④ 매장 상주·베스트셀러 브라우징(주제 무관) ✓
①특정 제목의 재고를 두지 않음(주제)✘ 없음
②진열대 브라우징에 의존 못해 다른 방법으로 수집✘ 없음
③정례적으로 카탈로그 배포·구매 가능 여부 문의✘ 없음
④매장을 오래 열어 관광객이 베스트셀러를 봄✔ 근거
⑤도달 범위가 배가·확장✘ 없음
공식주제에서 벗어난 소재·논리의 문장 1개

🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Most antiquarian and second-hand booksellers do not keep large stocks of any specific title, since each book they handle is usually unique.
대부분의 고서·중고 서적상은 특정 제목의 재고를 많이 두지 않는다, 그들이 다루는 각 책은 대개 유일하기 때문에. ← 주제
2
Full-time booksellers must, crucially, keep their shop premises open long hours so that passing tourists can browse the latest bestsellers.
전업 서적상은 무엇보다 매장을 오랜 시간 열어 두어야 한다 지나가는 관광객이 최신 베스트셀러를 둘러볼 수 있도록. ← 무관(정답)
④정답 매장 상주·관광객 베스트셀러 브라우징(주제 이탈) · ①재고 없음 ②다른 수집법 ③카탈로그 배포 ⑤도달 확장

유형 07 / 08 PART 1 · 유형별 훈련
36
글의 순서
36-37번대 · 실제 기출 11문항
유형 공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
이렇게 접근한다지시어(this·they)와 연결어(however·so), 시간 순서로 사슬을 잇는다.
STEP 1직접 풀기신호를 떠올리며 직접 형광펜을 치고 답을 고른다 (왼쪽 페이지)
STEP 2훈련 · 정답 칠🟡읽을 문장·🔴칠한 근거 + 노랑만으로 정답 도출 + 선지 판정
STEP 3직독직해같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 청크 대응 (오른쪽 페이지)
📢 이 유형 신호 리마인더
지시어 this·that연결어 However·So연대·순서
wbspread
37글의 순서 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Norms emerge in groups as a result of people conforming to the behavior of others. Thus, the start of a norm occurs when one person acts in a particular manner in a particular situation because she thinks she ought to. (C) Others may then conform to this behavior for a number of reasons. The person who performed the initial action may think that others ought to behave as she behaves in situations of this sort. (A) Thus, she may prescribe the behavior to them by uttering the norm statement in a prescriptive manner. Alternately, she may communicate that conformity is desired in other ways, such as by gesturing. In addition, she may threaten to sanction them for not behaving as she wishes. This will cause some to conform to her wishes and act as she acts. (B) But some others will not need to have the behavior prescribed to them. They will observe the regularity of behavior and decide on their own that they ought to conform. They may do so for either rational or moral reasons.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?
④ (B)⑤ (A)
🖍 이렇게 풀어요
1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지
📢 신호 리마인더
지시어 this·that연결어 However·So연대·순서

지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
37글의 순서STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #59
Norms emerge in groups as a result of people conforming to the behavior of others. 결과Thus, the start of a norm occurs when one person acts in a particular manner in a particular situation because she thinks she ought to. (C) Others may 연결then conform to this behavior for a number of reasons. The person who performed the initial action may think that others ought to behave as she behaves in situations of this sort. (A) 결과Thus, she may prescribe the behavior to them by uttering the norm statement in a prescriptive manner. Alternately, she may communicate that conformity is desired in other ways, such as by gesturing. 추가In addition, she may threaten to sanction them for not behaving as she wishes. This will cause 한정some to conform to her wishes and act as she acts. (B) 역접But some others will not need to have the behavior prescribed to them. They will observe the regularity of behavior and decide on their own that they ought to conform. They may do so for either rational or moral reasons.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①도입: 최초 행위자 '그녀'가 스스로 옳다고 여겨 행동 — — 이어질 대명사 she/others의 기준점
  2. 노랑②(C) 'The person who performed the initial action'이 others도 그렇게 해야 한다고 생각 — — 도입의 '그녀'를 그대로 받음 → 도입 바로 뒤
  3. 노랑③(A) 'Thus, she may prescribe ... to them' → some이 따름 — — (C)의 others=them, 규범을 '규정'하는 단계
  4. 노랑④(B) 'But some others will not need to have it prescribed' → 스스로 따름 — — (A)의 some(규정으로 따름)과 대조 → 마지막
→ 도입 → (C)others 등장 → (A)them에게 규정 → (B)But 규정 불필요한 이들 = ④ (C)-(A)-(B)
gesturing·rational/moral 예시(skip)는 배열 근거 아님.
패러프레이즈 one person acts → others → prescribe/But self-conform → (C)-(A)-(B) ✓
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 정반대
②(B)-(A)-(C)✘ 정반대
③(B)-(C)-(A)✘ 정반대
④(C)-(A)-(B)✔ 근거
⑤(C)-(B)-(A)✘ 정반대
공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
37글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Others may then conform to this behavior for a number of reasons.
그러면 다른 이들이 따를 수 있다 이 행동을 여러 이유로. ← 근거: 도입 뒤 others 첫 등장 (C)
2
Thus, she may prescribe the behavior to them by uttering the norm statement.
그리하여 그녀는 그 행동을 규정할 수 있다 그들에게 규범 진술을 말함으로써. ← 근거: them=(C)의 others (A)
3
But some others will not need to have the behavior prescribed to them.
그러나 또 다른 일부는 행동을 규정받을 필요가 없을 것이다 그들에게. ← 근거: But으로 (A)와 대조 (B)
④정답 (C)others→(A)them 규정→(B)But 자발 · ①②③⑤ 지시어 연결 어긋남
wbspread
37글의 순서 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Although there may be elements that trigger instinctive response, spectacle works primarily within a cultural frame and is subject to prevailing modes of perception that observers bring to bear. In saying that, we stress that creation of spectacle is neither a universal nor even an inevitable expression of culture, but is a strategy commonly used by people from many different cultures to address specific needs. The task of creating spectacle has been likened to creating new works of literature, where writers normally feel bound by the rules and traditions of their genre yet need to show sufficient originality to impress their audiences. By extension, spectacle is produced by working with the grain of a particular culture, blending the innovative with the established. Thus anyone deploying spectacle may draw on a repertoire of techniques and conventions developed from previous festivals or related activities, but imitation is not enough. Spectacle must also be spectacular. A spectacle has to amaze spectators, outdoing previous efforts if it is to become part of the collective memory of those who witnessed and participated in it.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?
④ (B)⑤ (A)

The task of creating spectacle has been 비유likened to creating new works of literature, where writers normally feel bound by the rules and traditions of their genre yet need to show sufficient originality to impress their audiences. 연결By extension, spectacle is produced by working with the grain of a particular culture, blending the innovative with the established. Thus anyone deploying spectacle may draw on a repertoire of techniques and conventions developed from previous festivals or related activities, 역접but imitation is not enough. Spectacle must 첨가also be spectacular. A spectacle has to amaze spectators, outdoing previous efforts if it is to become part of the collective memory of those who witnessed and participated in it.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 글: spectacle은 문화적 틀 안에서 작동 → (C) 'In saying that'가 이를 받아 '보편·필연이 아닌 전략'이라 부연 + 문학 비유 — — 지시어로 주어진 글과 (C) 연결
  2. 노랑②(A) 'By extension' 기존 관습을 활용하되 'imitation is not enough(모방만으론 부족)' — — 문학 비유의 '규칙 준수' 측면 확장
  3. 노랑③(B) 'must also be spectacular' 관객을 놀라게·이전을 능가 — — '모방 부족' 다음의 '독창성' 측면 → (A) 뒤
→ 주어진 글 → (C) → (A) → (B) = ④
(B)의 amaze/collective memory 부연은 배열에 영향 없음. 핵심은 지시어·연결어(In saying that·By extension·also·but).
패러프레이즈 In saying that · By extension · also · but imitation is not enough → (C)-(A)-(B) ✓
④(C)-(A)-(B)✔ 근거
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 연결오류
②(B)-(A)-(C)✘ 연결오류
③(B)-(C)-(A)✘ 연결오류
⑤(C)-(B)-(A)✘ 연결오류
공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
37글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
In saying that, we stress that creation of spectacle is neither a universal nor even an inevitable expression of culture, but is a strategy commonly used by people from many different cultures.
그렇게 말하면서 우리는 강조한다 스펙터클의 창출이 문화의 보편적이거나 필연적인 표현이 아니라, 여러 다른 문화의 사람들이 흔히 쓰는 전략임을. ← 지시어(C)
2
By extension, spectacle is produced by working with the grain of a particular culture, blending the innovative with the established, but imitation is not enough.
더 나아가, 스펙터클은 특정 문화의 결을 따라 작업함으로써 만들어지며, 혁신적인 것과 확립된 것을 뒤섞지만, 모방만으로는 충분하지 않다. ← 연결(A)
3
Spectacle must also be spectacular. A spectacle has to amaze spectators, outdoing previous efforts.
스펙터클은 또한 볼만해야 한다. 스펙터클은 관객을 놀라게 해야 하며, 이전의 시도들을 능가해야 한다. ← 첨가(B)
④정답 (C)-(A)-(B) · ①(A)-(C)-(B) ②(B)-(A)-(C) ③(B)-(C)-(A) ⑤(C)-(B)-(A)

In economics, there is a principle known as the sunk cost fallacy. The idea is that when you are invested and have ownership in something, you overvalue that thing. (B) This leads people to continue on paths or pursuits that should clearly be abandoned. (A) Sometimes, the smartest thing a person can do is quit. Although this is true, it has also become a tired and played-out argument. Sunk cost doesn’t always have to be a bad thing. (C) Actually, you can leverage this human tendency to your benefit.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?

In economics, there is a principle known as the sunk cost fallacy. The idea is that when you are invested and have ownership in something, you 정의overvalue that thing. (B) 지시어This leads people to continue on paths or pursuits that should clearly be abandoned. (A) Sometimes, the smartest thing a person can do is quit. 양보Although this is true, it has also become a tired and played-out argument. Sunk cost doesn’t always have to be a bad thing. (C) 연결어Actually, you can leverage this human tendency to your benefit.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 글: 투자하면 그것을 과대평가한다 → (B) This가 그 '과대평가'를 받아 '버려야 할 길을 계속 간다' — — 지시어 This = 앞의 overvalue
  2. 노랑②(B)의 나쁜 사례(관계·사업) 뒤 → (A) '그만두는 게 낫다'는 말은 맞지만 진부하다, Sunk cost가 늘 나쁜 건 아니다 — — 부정 사례 → 반전 도입
  3. 노랑③(A)의 반전을 → (C) Actually 이 성향을 이롭게 활용할 수 있다(트레이너 예) — — A의 '나쁘지만은 않다'를 구체화
→ 주어진 글 → (B) → (A) → (C) = ②
지시어 This·연결어 Actually가 순서를 고정한다.
패러프레이즈 overvalue → This / Sunk cost isn’t always bad → Actually leverage → (B)-(A)-(C) ✓
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 없음
②(B)-(A)-(C)✔ 근거
③(B)-(C)-(A)✘ 없음
④(C)-(A)-(B)✘ 없음
⑤(C)-(B)-(A)✘ 없음

A good example of chaos is the magnetic pendulum sold as an executive toy. It has four magnets arranged in a square at the base and a pendulum that swings back and forth between them. In order to produce chaos, the iteration has to be within what is called a nonlinear system. Nor are all nonlinear systems chaotic: to become so they need to be pushed beyond a certain point, called a bifurcation. Before that point is reached they may behave in a quite orderly fashion. Release the pendulum and note the magnets that it visits, and in what order. If the pendulum is released from the same position a second time, the pattern of movement may at first be the same but soon it will become completely different. In fact, the pattern of its movement is chaotic. No matter how much care is taken to start the pendulum in the same position, it will visit an entirely different set of points on the two occasions. Chaotic systems are generated by iteration, though not all iteration leads to chaos.

④ (B)⑤ (A)

A good example of chaos is the magnetic pendulum sold as an executive toy. It has four magnets arranged in a square at the base and a pendulum that swings back and forth between them. 인과In order to produce chaos, the iteration has to be within what is called a nonlinear system. 부정연결Nor are all nonlinear systems chaotic: to become so they need to be pushed beyond a certain point, called a bifurcation. Before that point is reached they may behave in a quite orderly fashion. Release the pendulum and note the magnets that it visits, and in what order. 조건If the pendulum is released from the same position a second time, the pattern of movement may at first be the same but soon it will become completely different. 강조In fact, the pattern of its movement is chaotic. 양보No matter how much care is taken to start the pendulum in the same position, it will visit an entirely different set of points on the two occasions. Chaotic systems are generated by iteration, 양보though not all iteration leads to chaos.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①도입은 진자 장난감을 설명 → 곧바로 (B) Release the pendulum(진자를 놓아 보라)로 이어짐 — — 장난감을 '작동시키는' 지시가 도입 바로 뒤에 옴
  2. 노랑②(B) '같은 위치에서 놓아도 곧 완전히 달라진다' → (C) No matter how much care…같은 위치라도 '전혀 다른 점들'을 방문 — — (B)의 결과를 (C)가 재진술하며 확장
  3. 노랑③(C) 끝 'iteration으로 생기지만 모든 iteration이 혼돈은 아님' → (A) 'In order to produce chaos, the iteration…' 로 연결 — — 반복(iteration)이라는 열쇠말이 (C)→(A)를 이음
→ 도입-(B)-(C)-(A): 작동→결과→일반화 = ③ (B)-(C)-(A)
회색(In fact·Nor 문장 등 부연)은 흐름 판정에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 same position → completely different → iteration → not all chaotic ✓
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 없음
②(B)-(A)-(C)✘ 없음
③(B)-(C)-(A)✔ 근거
④(C)-(A)-(B)✘ 없음

1

진자를 놓고 그것이 방문하는 자석들을 기록하라, 그리고 어떤 순서로인지도. ← (B) 시작

No matter how much care is taken to start the pendulum in the same position, it will visit an entirely different set of points on the two occasions.

In order to produce chaos, the iteration has to be within what is called a nonlinear system.
혼돈을 만들어내려면, 그 반복(iteration)이 있어야 한다 이른바 비선형 시스템 안에. ← (A) 연결
③정답 (B)-(C)-(A): 진자 작동→같은 위치라도 다른 결과→iteration으로 일반화 · ①②④⑤는 iteration 연결·지시 흐름이 끊김

(B) If the baby water flea is developing into an adult in water that includes the chemical signatures of creatures that prey on water fleas, it develops a helmet and spines to defend itself against predators. (A) That’s a clever trick, because producing spines and a helmet is costly, in terms of energy. (C) So it may well be that this plasticity is an adaptation: a trait that came to exist in a species because it contributed to reproductive fitness.

36글의 순서STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-수능 · #63
A fascinating species of water flea exhibits a kind of flexibility that evolutionary biologists call adaptive plasticity. (B) 조건If the baby water flea is developing into an adult in water that includes the chemical signatures of creatures that prey on water fleas, it develops a helmet and spines to defend itself against predators. (A) 지시어That’s a clever trick, because producing spines and a helmet is costly, in terms of energy. (C) 결론So it may well be that this plasticity is an adaptation: a trait that came to exist in a species because it contributed to reproductive fitness.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①도입: 물벼룩은 적응적 가소성이라는 유연성을 보인다 — 아직 그 유연성이 '무엇'인지는 미설명 — — (A)의 'That's a clever trick'이 받을 대상이 아직 없음
  2. 노랑②(B) 포식자 신호가 있으면 투구·가시를 만들고 없으면 안 만든다 (조건부 행동) — — If로 그 유연한 '방책'을 구체 서술 → B가 먼저
  3. 노랑③(A) ‘That’s a clever trick’ = B의 조건부 발달을 받음 (에너지 절약 이유) — — 지시어 That's → B 뒤
  4. 노랑④(C) ‘So … this plasticity is an adaptation’ 로 마무리 — — So: 결론 → 맨 뒤
→ 주어진 글 － (B) － (A) － (C) = ②
(A)의 'That's a clever trick'은 (B)의 조건부 행동을 가리키므로 B가 A보다 앞서야 함.
패러프레이즈 조건부 발달(B) → That’s a clever trick(A) → So adaptation(C) → (B)－(A)－(C) ✓
②(B) － (A) － (C)✔ 근거
①(A) － (C) － (B)✘ 없음
③(B) － (C) － (A)✘ 없음
④(C) － (A) － (B)✘ 없음
⑤(C) － (B) － (A)✘ 없음
공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
36글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
If the water includes chemical signatures of predators, it develops a helmet and spines.
물에 포식자의 화학적 신호가 있으면, 투구와 가시를 발달시킨다. ← 근거(B가 먼저)
2
That’s a clever trick, because producing spines and a helmet is costly.
그것은 영리한 방책인데, 가시와 투구를 만드는 데 비용이 크기 때문이다. ← 근거(That’s → B를 받음)
3
So it may well be that this plasticity is an adaptation.
그래서 아마도 이 가소성은 하나의 적응일 것이다. ← 결론
②정답 (B)－(A)－(C) · That’s→B, So→결론(C) · ①③④⑤ 지시어·연결 흐름 어긋남

🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
[주어진 글] The most commonly known form of results-based pricing is a practice called contingency pricing, used by lawyers. (C) Contingency pricing is the major way that personal injury and certain consumer cases are billed. In this approach, lawyers do not receive fees or payment until the case is settled, when they are paid a percentage of the money that the client receives. (A) Therefore, only an outcome in the client’s favor is compensated. From the client’s point of view, the pricing makes sense in part because most clients in these cases are unfamiliar with and possibly intimidated by law firms. Their biggest fears are high fees for a case that may take years to settle. (B) By using contingency pricing, clients are ensured that they pay no fees until they receive a settlement. In these and other instances of contingency pricing, the economic value of the service is hard to determine before the service, and providers develop a price that allows them to share the risks and rewards of delivering value to the buyer.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?
① (A) －(C) －(B)② (B) －(A) －(C)③ (B) －(C) －(A)④ (C) －(A) －(B)⑤ (C) －(B) －(A)
🖍 이렇게 풀어요
1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지
📢 신호 리마인더
지시어 this·that연결어 However·So연대·순서

  • 근거 신호 문장을 찾아 칠했나?
  • 예시·양보절은 회색으로 넘겼나?
  • 지문 단어 그대로 쓴 선지부터 지웠나?

37글의 순서STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-수능 · #64
[주어진 글] The most commonly known form of results-based pricing is a practice called 도입contingency pricing, used by lawyers. (C) Contingency pricing is the major way that personal injury and certain consumer cases are billed. 지시어In this approach, lawyers do not receive fees or payment until the case is settled, when they are paid a percentage of the money that the client receives. (A) 인과Therefore, only an outcome in the client’s favor is compensated. From the client’s point of view, the pricing makes sense in part because most clients in these cases are unfamiliar with and possibly intimidated by law firms. Their biggest fears are high fees for a case that may take years to settle. (B) By using contingency pricing, clients are ensured that they pay no fees until they receive a settlement. 결론In these and other instances of contingency pricing, the economic value of the service is hard to determine before the service, and providers develop a price that allows them to share the risks and rewards of delivering value to the buyer.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 글: 성과 기반 가격의 대표 = 성공보수(contingency pricing) 소개 — — 개념어를 처음 던짐
  2. 노랑②(C): In this approach 그 방식을 구체 설명 — 사건 해결 전엔 수임료 없음, 승소 시 비율로 받음 — — 주어진 글의 개념을 곧바로 풀이 → 맨 앞
  3. 노랑③(A): Therefore 의뢰인에게 유리한 결과만 보상 → 의뢰인의 고액 수임료 공포 언급 — — (C)의 '비율 지급' 결과로 이어짐
  4. 노랑④(B): 그 공포 해결 — 합의 전엔 수임료 없음, 위험·보상 공유로 마무리 — — (A)의 두려움에 대한 답 → 마지막
→ 흐름: 주어진 글 → (C) → (A) → (B) = ④ (C)-(A)-(B)
(C)의 'In this approach'가 주어진 글을 받고, (A)의 'Therefore'가 (C)를 받는 인과 사슬이 핵심.
패러프레이즈 In this approach → Therefore → By using contingency pricing → (C)-(A)-(B) ✓
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 없음
②(B)-(A)-(C)✘ 없음
③(B)-(C)-(A)✘ 없음
④(C)-(A)-(B)✔ 근거
⑤(C)-(B)-(A)✘ 없음
공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
37글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
In this approach, lawyers do not receive fees until the case is settled, when they are paid a percentage of the money the client receives.
이 방식에서는, 변호사가 사건이 해결될 때까지 수임료를 안 받고, 그때 의뢰인이 받는 돈의 일정 비율을 받는다. ← (C) 주어진 글 이어받음
2
Therefore, only an outcome in the client’s favor is compensated. Their biggest fears are high fees for a case that may take years.
따라서, 의뢰인에게 유리한 결과만 보상된다. 그들의 가장 큰 두려움은 오래 걸릴 사건의 높은 수임료다. ← (A) Therefore로 (C) 이어받음
3
By using contingency pricing, clients pay no fees until they receive a settlement, and providers share the risks and rewards.
성공보수제를 씀으로써, 의뢰인은 합의를 받기 전엔 수임료를 안 내고, 제공자는 위험과 보상을 나눈다. ← (B) (A)의 두려움 해소 → 마무리
④정답 (C)-(A)-(B): 개념 설명→Therefore 결과→두려움 해소 · ①②③⑤ 지시어(In this approach·Therefore) 연결 어긋남

36글의 순서 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Traditionally, when teachers teach writing, they assign topics for students to write on; perhaps they do a bit of brainstorming about the topic during a pre-writing phase, and then have students write about the topic without interruption. (C) Subsequently, teachers collect and evaluate what students have written. Such instruction is very ‘product-oriented;’ there is no involvement of the teacher in the act or ‘process’ of writing. (A) In process writing, on the other hand, students may initially brainstorm ideas about a topic and begin writing, but then they have repeated conferences with the teacher and the other students, during which they receive feedback on their writing up to that point, make revisions, based on the feedback they receive, and carry on writing. (B) In this way, students learn to view their writing as someone else’s reading and to improve both the expression of meaning and the form of their writing as they draft and redraft. Process writing shifts the emphasis in teaching writing from evaluation to revision.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?
④ (B)⑤ (A)

36글의 순서STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2026-09 · #65
도입Traditionally, when teachers teach writing, they assign topics for students to write on; perhaps they do a bit of brainstorming about the topic during a pre-writing phase, and then have students write about the topic without interruption. (C) 연결Subsequently, teachers collect and evaluate what students have written. 지시어Such instruction is very ‘product-oriented;’ there is no involvement of the teacher in the act or ‘process’ of writing. (A) In process writing, 역접on the other hand, students may initially brainstorm ideas about a topic and begin writing, but then they have repeated conferences with the teacher and the other students, during which they receive feedback on their writing up to that point, make revisions, based on the feedback they receive, and carry on writing. (B) 지시어In this way, students learn to view their writing as someone else’s reading and to improve both the expression of meaning and the form of their writing as they draft and redraft. Process writing shifts the emphasis in teaching writing from evaluation to revision.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 글: 전통적 방식 — 주제를 주고 중단 없이 쓰게 함 — — 쓰기 '전' 단계까지 서술, 그 다음이 필요
  2. 노랑②(C) Subsequently(그 후) 교사가 걷어 평가 → '결과 중심(product-oriented)' — — 전통 방식의 마무리 = 주어진 글 바로 뒤
  3. 노랑③(A) on the other hand 과정 중심 쓰기 소개 → (B) In this way 그 효과 정리 — — C(전통)와 대조되는 A, 그 방식을 받는 B
→ 주어진 글 → (C) → (A) → (B) 이므로 순서는 ④ (C)-(A)-(B)
'Such/In this way' 지시어가 앞 문장을 받으므로 위치가 고정됨.
패러프레이즈 Subsequently·on the other hand·In this way → 주어진글-C-A-B 흐름 ✓
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 없음
②(B)-(A)-(C)✘ 없음
③(B)-(C)-(A)✘ 없음
④(C)-(A)-(B)✔ 근거
⑤(A)✘ 없음
공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
36글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
(C) Subsequently, teachers collect and evaluate what students have written.
(C) 그 후, 교사가 걷어서 평가한다 학생이 쓴 것을. ← 근거
2
(A) In process writing, on the other hand, they have repeated conferences and carry on writing.
(A) 반면, 과정 중심 쓰기에서는, 반복적으로 협의를 하고 계속 이어서 쓴다. ← 근거
3
(B) In this way, students learn to view their writing as someone else’s reading.
(B) 이런 식으로, 학생은 자기 글을 보는 법을 배운다 남의 읽을거리로. ← 근거
④정답 (C)-(A)-(B): 전통 마무리(C)→반면 과정중심(A)→이런 식으로(B) · ①②③⑤지시어 연결이 끊김

37글의 순서 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Perhaps at some point you have seen some mathematical writing and not understood it. (B) You would not be the first; rest assured, even professional mathematicians sometimes have to rely on discussions with colleagues to properly understand problems they are looking at. But how do you recognise some writing is mathematical in the first place? (A) The complicated notations that might spring to mind ― all those strange dashes, squiggles and letters ― are obvious signs, but a lot of those are really quite modern. Mathematics had been going on for a long time before the dashes and squiggles were invented. (C) Put simply, there has to be something mathematical going on for us to say that it is mathematics. And if we are dealing with writing from a very distant past, in a language that is not familiar to us, from a time even before recorded language, that can be sometimes difficult to recognise.

🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 글: 수학 글을 보고 이해 못 한 적 있을 것 — — 도입

  3. 노랑③(A) 떠오르는 복잡한 기호가 표시지만, 그건 사실 현대의 것 — — (B)의 질문(어떻게 아나)에 답 시작
  4. 노랑④(C) 간단히 말해 수학적인 무언가가 있어야 = 옛 기록은 알아보기 어려움 — — 정리·결론
→ 흐름: 도입 → (B) 질문 → (A) 기호 단서 → (C) 정리 = ② (B)-(A)-(C)
(B)의 ‘첫 문장이 주어진 글의 not understood’를 받는 점이 핵심 고리.

36글의 순서 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
The fossil record provides evidence of evolution. The story the fossils tell is one of change. Creatures existed in the past that are no longer with us. Sequential changes are found in many fossils showing the change of certain features over time from a common ancestor. (C) Apart from demonstrating that evolution did occur, the fossil record also provides tests of the predictions made from evolutionary theory. For example, the theory predicts that single-celled organisms evolved before multicelled organisms. (B) The fossil record supports this prediction ―multicelled organisms are found in layers of earth millions of years after the first appearance of single-celled organisms. Note that the possibility always remains that the opposite could be found. (A) If multicelled organisms were indeed found to have evolved before single-celled organisms, then the theory of evolution would be rejected. A good scientific theory always allows for the possibility of rejection. The fact that we have not found such a case in countless examinations of the fossil record strengthens the case for evolutionary theory.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?

The fact that we have not found 지시어such a case in countless examinations of the fossil record strengthens the case for evolutionary theory.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①(C) 화석 기록은 진화 이론의 예측을 검증한다 → 예: 단세포가 다세포보다 먼저 진화 — — '이 예측(this prediction)'의 정체를 먼저 제시
  2. 노랑②(B) 화석 기록이 이 예측을 뒷받침 → 그런데 '반대(the opposite)도 발견될 수 있다'고 복선 — — 'this prediction'은 (C)의 예측을 받음, 마지막에 반대 경우를 언급
  3. 노랑③(A) 만약 다세포가 먼저 진화한 것으로 밝혀지면(=그 반대) 이론은 폐기될 것 — — (B)가 남긴 '반대 경우'를 If로 이어받아 마무리
→ 예측 제시(C) → 뒷받침·복선(B) → 그 반대 가정(A)으로 마무리 = ⑤ (C)-(B)-(A)
도입부(화석=변화의 기록)와 마지막 부연은 순서 판정에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 this prediction·the opposite → (C)의 예측·(A)의 If절 ✓
⑤(C) ― (B) ― (A)✔ 근거
①(A) ― (C) ― (B)✘ 없음
②(B) ― (A) ― (C)✘ 없음
③(B) ― (C) ― (A)✘ 없음
④(C) ― (A) ― (B)✘ 없음
공식지시어·연결어(this·however·연대)로 흐름 잇기
36글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
For example, the theory predicts that single-celled organisms evolved before multicelled organisms.
예를 들어, 그 이론은 예측한다 단세포 생물이 다세포 생물보다 먼저 진화했다고. ← (C)의 예측
2
The fossil record supports this prediction ―multicelled organisms are found in layers of earth millions of years after the first appearance of single-celled organisms.
화석 기록은 이 예측을 뒷받침한다 ―즉 다세포 생물이 지층에서 발견된다 수백만 년 뒤에 단세포 생물이 처음 출현한 것보다. ← (B)가 (C)를 받음
3
If multicelled organisms were indeed found to have evolved before single-celled organisms, then the theory of evolution would be rejected.
만약 다세포 생물이 실제로 진화한 것으로 밝혀진다면 단세포 생물보다 먼저, 그러면 진화 이론은 폐기될 것이다. ← (A)가 (B)의 '반대'를 이어받음
⑤정답 (C)예측 제시 → (B)뒷받침·'반대'복선 → (A)'만약 반대라면'으로 마무리 · ①②③④ 지시어(this prediction·the opposite) 연결이 깨짐
wbspread
36글의 순서 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Negotiation can be defined as an attempt to explore and reconcile conflicting positions in order to reach an acceptable outcome. (C) Whatever the nature of the outcome, which may actually favour one party more than another, the purpose of negotiation is the identification of areas of common interest and conflict. In this sense, depending on the intentions of the parties, the areas of common interest may be clarified, refined and given negotiated form and substance. (A) Areas of difference can and do frequently remain, and will perhaps be the subject of future negotiations, or indeed remain irreconcilable. In those instances in which the parties have highly antagonistic or polarised relations, the process is likely to be dominated by the exposition, very often in public, of the areas of conflict. (B) In these and sometimes other forms of negotiation, negotiation serves functions other than reconciling conflicting interests. These will include delay, publicity, diverting attention or seeking intelligence about the other party and its negotiating position.
주어진 글 다음에 이어질 글의 순서로 가장 적절한 것은?
④ (B)⑤ (A)
🖍 이렇게 풀어요
1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지
📢 신호 리마인더
지시어 this·that연결어 However·So연대·순서

  • 근거 신호 문장을 찾아 칠했나?
  • 예시·양보절은 회색으로 넘겼나?
  • 지문 단어 그대로 쓴 선지부터 지웠나?

36글의 순서STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #68
Negotiation can be 정의defined as an attempt to explore and reconcile conflicting positions in order to reach an acceptable outcome. (C) Whatever the nature of the 반복어outcome, which may actually favour one party more than another, the purpose of negotiation is the identification of areas of common interest and conflict. In this sense, depending on the intentions of the parties, the areas of common interest may be clarified, refined and given negotiated form and substance. (A) 반복어Areas of difference can and do frequently remain, and will perhaps be the subject of future negotiations, or indeed remain irreconcilable. In those instances in which the parties have highly 상황제시antagonistic or polarised relations, the process is likely to be dominated by the exposition, very often in public, of the areas of conflict. (B) 지시어In these and sometimes other forms of negotiation, negotiation serves functions other than reconciling conflicting interests. These will include delay, publicity, diverting attention or seeking intelligence about the other party and its negotiating position.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 문장: 협상은 상충하는 입장을 조정해 수용 가능한 ‘결과(outcome)’에 이르는 것 — — (C)의 ‘Whatever the nature of the outcome’가 이 결과를 이어받음
  2. 노랑②(C) 협상의 목적 = 공통 이익과 ‘갈등(conflict)’ 영역의 확인 — — (A)의 ‘Areas of difference’가 이 갈등/차이를 이어받고, 적대·공개적 대립 상황 제시
  3. 노랑③(A) 적대적·양극화된 관계의 공개적 대립 — — (B)의 지시어 ‘In these … forms of negotiation’이 이 상황들을 가리킴
→ outcome→conflict→In these로 이어지면: (C)-(A)-(B) = ④
각 단락 둘째 문장(부연·열거)은 회색·스킵해도 링크에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 acceptable outcome·areas of conflict·antagonistic public exposition → (C)-(A)-(B) ✓
①(A)-(C)-(B)✘ 없음
②(B)-(A)-(C)✘ 없음
③(B)-(C)-(A)✘ 없음
④(C)-(A)-(B)✔ 근거
⑤(C)-(B)-(A)✘ 없음

36글의 순서STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Negotiation can be defined as an attempt to explore and reconcile conflicting positions in order to reach an acceptable outcome.
협상은 ~하려는 시도로 정의될 수 있다 상충하는 입장을 탐색·조정하여 수용 가능한 결과에 이르려는.
2
Whatever the nature of the outcome, the purpose of negotiation is the identification of areas of common interest and conflict.
결과의 성격이 어떠하든, 협상의 목적은 공통 이익과 갈등의 영역을 확인하는 것이다. ← 근거
3
In these … forms of negotiation, negotiation serves functions other than reconciling conflicting interests.
이러한 … 형태의 협상에서, 협상은 ~ 기능을 수행한다 상충하는 이익을 조정하는 것 외의. ← 지시어 근거
④(C)-(A)-(B) outcome→conflict→In these로 연결 · ①②③⑤ 지시어·흐름 불일치

If we take an evolutionary look at our beginnings, we see a life in which high levels of physical activity were required for survival. (C) Even one century ago, most people needed to be physically active to work, to travel, and to take care of homes and families. Our modern world has engineered such activity out of our lives. (A) There are fewer manual jobs, we do not need to travel on foot, we do not need to hunt and harvest for our food, and many domestic chores have been mechanized. While these changes have created many benefits for our longevity and quality of life, they have also created many problems. (B) Lack of sufficient physical activity has now been linked to at least 17 unhealthy conditions, almost all of which are chronic diseases or considered risk factors for chronic diseases. Adrianne Hardman has summarized this serious situation for public health: “Physical inactivity is a waste of human potential for health and well-being.”

④ (B)⑤ (A)

If we take an evolutionary look at our 도입beginnings, we see a life in which high levels of physical activity were required for survival. 🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 글: 진화적으로 많은 활동이 필요했다 → (C) '한 세기 전에도' 활동적이어야 했다 — — beginnings→one century ago로 시간 흐름 연결
  2. 노랑②(C) 끝 '현대가 활동을 없앴다' → (A) 이런 변화(육체노동 감소 등) 구체화 — — these changes가 (C)의 modern world를 받음

→ 흐름은 주어진 글-(C)-(A)-(B) = ④
인용문(Hardman)은 마무리 부연, 순서 판단엔 불필요.

🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Even one century ago, most people needed to be physically active to work, to travel, and to take care of homes.
한 세기 전만 해도, 대부분의 사람은 신체적으로 활동적이어야 했다 일하고, 이동하고, 가정을 돌보기 위해. ← (C) 근거

There are fewer manual jobs, we do not need to travel on foot, and many domestic chores have been mechanized.

While these changes have created many benefits, they have also created many problems.
이런 변화가 많은 이점을 만들었지만, 그것은 많은 문제도 만들어냈다. ← (A)→(B) 연결
④정답 주어진글-(C)-(A)-(B) · these changes=(C)의 현대화 · many problems→(B) 활동부족·질병

문장 삽입
38-39번대 · 실제 기출 11문항
유형 공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
이렇게 접근한다주어진 문장의 지시어가 '갑자기 튀는' 논리 공백 지점을 찾는다.
STEP 1직접 풀기신호를 떠올리며 직접 형광펜을 치고 답을 고른다 (왼쪽 페이지)
STEP 2훈련 · 정답 칠🟡읽을 문장·🔴칠한 근거 + 노랑만으로 정답 도출 + 선지 판정
STEP 3직독직해같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 청크 대응 (오른쪽 페이지)
📢 이 유형 신호 리마인더

39문장 삽입 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
[삽입 문장] As a result, they are fit and grow better, but they aren’t particularly long-lived. When trees grow together, nutrients and water can be optimally divided among them all so that each tree can grow into the best tree it can be. If you “help” individual trees by getting rid of their supposed competition, the remaining trees are bereft. They send messages out to their neighbors unsuccessfully, because nothing remains but stumps. Every tree now grows on its own, giving rise to great differences in productivity. (①) Some individuals photosynthesize like mad until sugar positively bubbles along their trunk. (②) This is because a tree can be only as strong as the forest that surrounds it. (③) And there are now a lot of losers in the forest. (④) Weaker members, who would once have been supported by the stronger ones, suddenly fall behind. (⑤) Whether the reason for their decline is their location and lack of nutrients, a passing sickness, or genetic makeup, they now fall prey to insects and fungi.
글의 흐름으로 보아, 주어진 문장이 들어가기에 가장 적절한 곳은?

39문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-06 · #70
[삽입 문장] 결과As a result, they are fit and grow better, but they aren’t particularly long-lived. When trees grow together, nutrients and water can be optimally divided among them all so that each tree can grow into the best tree it can be. If you “help” individual trees by getting rid of their supposed competition, the remaining trees are bereft. They send messages out to their neighbors unsuccessfully, because nothing remains but stumps. Every tree now grows on its own, giving rise to great differences in productivity. (①) Some individuals photosynthesize like mad until sugar positively bubbles along their trunk. (②) 인과This is because a tree can be only as strong as the forest that surrounds it. (③) And there are now a lot of losers in the forest. (④) Weaker members, who would once have been supported by the stronger ones, suddenly fall behind. (⑤) Whether the reason for their decline is their location and lack of nutrients, a passing sickness, or genetic makeup, they now fall prey to insects and fungi.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①①앞: 어떤 나무는 미친 듯 광합성해 줄기에 당이 넘침 (강자) — — they가 가리킬 주어(개별 나무)를 마련
  2. 노랑②삽입: 'As a result, they는 튼튼하지만 오래 못 산다' — — they=①의 강자, 결과로 '오래 못 삶'을 도입
  3. 노랑③②: 'This is because 나무는 숲만큼만 강할 수 있다' — — This=바로 앞 '오래 못 산다'의 이유 → 삽입문이 앞에 있어야 성립
→ ①(강자 등장) → 삽입(they 결과) → ②(This 이유) : 논리 공백은 ②
③④⑤(약자들의 몰락 예시·skip)는 삽입 위치 판단과 무관.
패러프레이즈 photosynthesize like mad → As a result they aren’t long-lived → This is because → 삽입 위치 ② ✓
①① 광합성 개체 등장 앞✘ 없음
②② they(강자)→오래 못 삶, This로 이유 연결✔ 근거
③③ a lot of losers 앞✘ 없음
④④ Weaker members fall behind 앞✘ 없음
⑤⑤ 쇠락 원인 열거 앞✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
39문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Some individuals photosynthesize like mad until sugar bubbles along their trunk.
일부 개체는 미친 듯이 광합성한다 줄기에 당이 넘칠 때까지. ← 삽입문의 they가 가리킬 대상
2
As a result, they are fit and grow better, but they aren’t particularly long-lived.
그 결과 그들은 건강하고 더 잘 자라지만, 특별히 오래 살지는 못한다. ← 근거: 삽입 문장
3
This is because a tree can be only as strong as the forest that surrounds it.
이는 ~때문이다 나무는 딱 그만큼만 강할 수 있기 자신을 둘러싼 숲만큼. ← 근거: This=삽입문의 '오래 못 삶'
②정답 As a result의 they=①강자, ②This가 결과를 받음 · ①③④⑤ 지시 연결 공백 없음

39문장 삽입 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
At the next step in the argument, however, the analogy breaks down. Misprints in a book or in any written message usually have a negative impact on the content, sometimes (literally) fatally. The displacement of a comma, for instance, may be a matter of life and death. Similarly most mutations have harmful consequences for the organism in which they occur, meaning that they reduce its reproductive fitness. Occasionally, however, a mutation may occur that increases the fitness of the organism, just as an accidental failure to reproduce the text of the first edition might provide more accurate or updated information. A favorable mutation is going to be more heavily represented in the next generation, since the organism in which it occurred will have more offspring and mutations are transmitted to the offspring. By contrast, there is no mechanism by which a book that accidentally corrects the mistakes of the first edition will tend to sell better.
글의 흐름으로 보아, 주어진 문장이 들어가기에 가장 적절한 곳은?
① ) The displacement of a comma, for instance, may be a② ) Similarly most mutations have③ )④ ) A favorable mutation⑤ ) By

1묻는 문장부터 읽고 '무엇을 묻나' 파악
2역접·한정·결론 신호 문장만 🟡, 예시는 넘기기
3지문어 복사 선지 소거 → 바꿔 말한 선지

39문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #71
At the next step in the argument, 역접however, the analogy breaks down. Misprints in a book or in any written message usually have a negative impact on the content, sometimes (literally) fatally. The displacement of a comma, for instance, may be a matter of life and death. 유추Similarly most mutations have harmful consequences for the organism in which they occur, meaning that they reduce its reproductive fitness. Occasionally, 역접however, a mutation may occur that increases the fitness of the organism, just as an accidental failure to reproduce the text of the first edition might provide more accurate or updated information. A favorable mutation is going to be more heavily represented in the next generation, 이유since the organism in which it occurred will have more offspring and mutations are transmitted to the offspring. 대조By contrast, there is no mechanism by which a book that accidentally corrects the mistakes of the first edition will tend to sell better.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①③ 문장: 가끔 적응도를 높이는 돌연변이가 생긴다 — — '유리한 돌연변이'라는 새 소재가 여기서 처음 등장
  2. 노랑②삽입문: A favorable mutation은 다음 세대에 더 많이 나타난다 — — 'A favorable mutation'은 바로 앞 ③의 '적응도를 높이는 돌연변이'를 받는 지시
  3. 노랑③⑤ By contrast: 책은 실수를 고쳐도 더 팔릴 기제가 없다 — — 앞의 '유리한 돌연변이는 확산된다'와 대조되어야 성립
→ '유리한 돌연변이 등장(③)→그것이 다음 세대에 퍼짐(삽입)→책은 그런 기제 없음(⑤ By contrast)'의 연결이 성립하는 자리 = ④
①~③(도입·예시)에는 '유리한 돌연변이'가 아직 안 나와 삽입문의 지시 대상이 없음.
패러프레이즈 a mutation may occur that increases the fitness → A favorable mutation ✓
①① Misprints ~ / The displacement of a comma✘ 없음
②② for instance / Similarly most mutations✘ 없음
③③ Similarly / Occasionally however✘ 없음
④④ 유리한 돌연변이 등장(③) → 다음 세대 확산(삽입) → By contrast(⑤)✔ 근거
⑤⑤ By contrast / (책 비교로 이미 넘어감)✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
39문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
Occasionally, however, a mutation may occur that increases the fitness of the organism,
가끔 그러나 돌연변이가 생길 수 있다 유기체의 적응도를 높이는, ← 삽입 직전(③)
2
A favorable mutation is going to be more heavily represented in the next generation, since the organism in which it occurred will have more offspring
유리한 돌연변이는 다음 세대에 더 많이 나타나게 된다, 그것이 생긴 유기체가 더 많은 자손을 가질 것이므로 ← 삽입문장
3
By contrast, there is no mechanism by which a book that accidentally corrects the mistakes will tend to sell better.
그에 반해, 기제가 전혀 없다 우연히 실수를 바로잡은 책이 더 잘 팔리게 되는 ← 삽입 직후(⑤)
④정답 '적응도 높이는 돌연변이'(③) 뒤에서 'A favorable mutation'이 이어지고 ⑤ By contrast가 대조를 이룸 · ①②③엔 '유리한 돌연변이'가 아직 없음 · ⑤는 이미 책 대조로 넘어간 자리

38문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2026-06 · #72
Research finds that people show a strong visceral interest in and desire to approach and own cute-looking and beautiful (elegant) designs. ( ① ) 역접However, cute and beautiful designs elicit two very different motivations. ( ② ) A cute product or package design elicits a nurturing motivation ― a desire to take care of and keep the product, to hold it dear to our hearts and never let it go. ( ③ ) The beautiful product or package design elicits a self-expressive, or signaling, motivation ― a desire to express oneself to others through product ownership. ( ④ ) Certain types of cute products can be associated with a 부정전환lack of sophistication or seriousness, which can reduce performance expectations (lowering perceived enablement benefits). ( ⑤ ) Beautiful-looking designs may 부정not attract attention over time because people become desensitized to
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 문장: '하지만 귀엽고 아름다운 디자인에도 단점이 있다' — also + downsides(복수) — — 뒤에 '단점' 두 가지가 이어져야 함
  2. 노랑②②③은 두 동기(양육·자기표현)로 아직 긍정 서술 → ④에서 갑자기 '세련미 부족·성능 기대 저하'라는 단점이 등장 — — ③→④ 사이 연결 없이 부정으로 급전환 = 논리 공백
→ '단점도 있다'는 총론 문장을 ④에 넣으면 ④(귀여움 단점)·⑤(아름다움 단점)를 묶어 준다 = ④
①의 However는 '동기 두 개'를 이끌 뿐 단점을 이끌지 않음(함정 ①).
패러프레이즈 But ... also have downsides → ④ 자리: 긍정(동기) → 부정(단점) 전환점 ✓
①① However 앞 — 동기 서술 전✘ 없음
②② 양육 동기 앞✘ 없음
③③ 자기표현 동기 앞✘ 없음
④④ 단점 서술 앞(공백)✔ 근거
⑤⑤ 아름다움 단점 앞✘ 범위초과
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점

1
The beautiful design elicits a self-expressive motivation.
아름다운 디자인은 이끌어낸다 자기표현의 동기를. ← ③=긍정(동기)

④정답 '단점도 있다'(also+복수) → ④⑤ 두 단점을 묶음 · ①However는 동기 유도 ②③긍정 동기 서술 ⑤이미 단점 진행 중

39문장 삽입 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
For thousands of years in the West, red was the only color worthy of that name, the only true color. As much on the historical as hierarchical level, it exceeded all others. Not that they did not exist, but they had to wait a long time before they were considered colors and then played a comparable role in material culture, social codes, and systems of thought. It was with red that humans did their first color experiments, achieved their first successes, and then constructed a chromatic universe. Here the lexicon seems in keeping with pictorial practices and coloring techniques. In certain languages, the same word can mean “red” or simply “colored,” depending on the context, such as coloratus in classical Latin or colorado in modern Castilian. In other languages, the words meaning “red” and “beautiful” share a common root; for example, that is the case in Russian, in which the terms krasnyy (red) and krasivy (beautiful) belong to the same lexical family.

39문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2027-06 · #73
For thousands of years in the West, red was the 한정only color worthy of that name, the only true color. As much on the historical as hierarchical level, it exceeded all others. Not that they did not exist, but they had to wait a long time before they were considered colors and then played a comparable role in material culture, social codes, and systems of thought. 강조구문It was with red that humans did their first color experiments, achieved their first successes, and then constructed a chromatic universe. 지시Here the lexicon seems in keeping with pictorial practices and coloring techniques. In certain languages, the same word can mean “red” or simply “colored,” depending on the context, such as coloratus in classical Latin or colorado in modern Castilian. In other languages, the words meaning “red” and “beautiful” share a common root; for example, that is the case in Russian, in which the terms krasnyy (red) and krasivy (beautiful) belong to the same lexical family.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①삽입 문장은 '빨강 안에서 팔레트를 다양화하고 색조를 만든 것을 가장 오래된 색채 용어(color terms)가 보여준다' — — 새 화제 '색채 용어(=어휘)'를 처음 도입
  2. 노랑②③ 뒤 문장: 'Here the lexicon(여기서 그 어휘)는 회화적 관행과 부합해 보인다' — — 'the lexicon'이 가리킬 대상(=color terms)이 앞에 있어야 함
  3. 노랑③③ 앞은 'red로 첫 실험…' → 삽입문 'It was also… reds'가 병렬로 자연스레 연결 — — 'also'가 앞 문장과 짝을 이룸
→ 'color terms'를 삽입문이 처음 꺼내야 뒤의 'the lexicon'이 성립 = ③
①②④⑤ 자리엔 'lexicon'을 미리 가리킬 대상이 없어 논리 공백이 생기지 않음.
패러프레이즈 the oldest known color terms → Here the lexicon ✓
①① Not that they did not exist 앞✘ 없음
②② It was with red 앞✘ 없음
③③ Here the lexicon 앞✔ 근거
④④ In certain languages 앞✘ 없음
⑤⑤ In other languages 앞✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
39문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
It was also within the range of reds that they learned early on to diversify the palette and to produce varied tones and shades, as the oldest known color terms demonstrate.
그것은 또한 빨강의 범위 안에서였다 그들이 일찍이 팔레트를 다양화하는 법을 배운 것은 그리고 다양한 색조와 명암을 만들어내는 법을, 가장 오래된 색채 용어들이 보여주듯이. ← 삽입 문장
2
Here the lexicon seems in keeping with pictorial practices and coloring techniques.
여기서 그 어휘는 ~와 부합하는 것으로 보인다 회화적 관행 및 채색 기법과. ← ③ 뒤 근거
③정답 삽입문이 'color terms(어휘)'를 처음 도입 → 뒤 문장 'the lexicon' 성립 · ①②④⑤는 지시어를 받쳐줄 대상 공백이 없음

39문장 삽입 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
[삽입문] They might be sitting still as they imagine all of this walking back and forth along their path; but they are reliving, at least in their imagination, the movement of their feet. Researchers are studying how our everyday physical experiences in the world contribute to our understanding of mathematical concepts. (①) The experience of walking along a path, for example, can be a metaphor for thinking about arithmetic. (②) The path starts at some point 0 and as children walk along, every step takes them 1 unit further from the starting point; they can even take half steps or skip along two steps at a time. (③) If they want to imagine what it might mean to add 5 and 9, they could think of first walking 9 steps and then walking 5 more. (④) But that also helps them think about what 14－3 might mean because they can imagine walking backwards. (⑤) If they close their eyes, they might even imagine the shape of the path, the smell of the trees, and the sound that is made when they step on the dried leaves.

지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
39문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2026-06 · #74
[삽입문] They might be sitting still as they imagine 지시어all of this walking back and forth along their path; 역접but they are reliving, at least in their imagination, the movement of their feet. Researchers are studying how our everyday physical experiences in the world contribute to our understanding of mathematical concepts. (①) The experience of walking along a path, for example, can be a metaphor for thinking about arithmetic. (②) The path starts at some point 0 and as children walk along, every step takes them 1 unit further from the starting point; they can even take half steps or skip along two steps at a time. (③) 조건If they want to imagine what it might mean to add 5 and 9, they could think of first walking 9 steps and then walking 5 more. (④) 역접But that also helps them think about what 14－3 might mean because they can imagine walking backwards. (⑤) 조건If they close their eyes, they might even imagine the shape of the path, the smell of the trees, and the sound that is made when they step on the dried leaves.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①①~⑤: 걷기(더하기·빼기·반걸음), 그리고 ⑤ 눈을 감고 길의 모양·냄새·소리까지 상상하기까지 걷기 상상이 누적된다 — — 상상 속 걷기가 계속 쌓임
  2. 노랑②삽입문: ‘이 모든 걷기(all of this walking)를 상상하며 가만히 앉아 있다’ = 앞의 걷기 상상 전체를 받는 지시어 — — 'all of this'는 ①~⑤를 총괄 → ⑤ 뒤라야 함
  3. 노랑③‘가만히 앉아 있지만 상상 속에서 발의 움직임을 되살린다’ = ⑤의 감각적 상상과 이어지는 마무리 — — but: 정지 vs 상상 속 움직임의 대비로 글을 닫음
→ ⑤ 뒤에서 ‘all of this walking’이 앞 내용 전체를 받아 마무리 = ⑤
삽입문이 ⑤ 앞에 오면 ‘all of this walking’이 받을 걷기 상상이 충분히 누적되지 않는다.
패러프레이즈 all of this walking back and forth·reliving the movement of their feet → ⑤ 뒤 삽입 ✓
⑤⑤ 뒤 (If they close their eyes … 다음)✔ 근거
①① The experience of walking 앞✘ 없음
②② The path starts at 0 앞✘ 없음
③③ If they want to imagine 앞✘ 없음
④④ But that also helps 앞✘ 없음

1

눈을 감으면, 길의 모양·냄새·소리까지 상상할 수 있다. ← ⑤ 자리

There’s a reason for that: traditionally, park designers attempted to create such a feeling by planting tall trees at park boundaries, building stone walls, and constructing other means of partition. Parks take the shape demanded by the cultural concerns of their time. Once parks are in place, they are no inert stage ― their purposes and meanings are made and remade by planners and by park users. Moments of park creation are particularly telling, however, for they reveal and actualize ideas about nature and its relationship to urban society. ( ①) Indeed, what distinguishes a park from the broader category of public space is the representation of nature that parks are meant to embody. ( ②) Public spaces include parks, concrete plazas, sidewalks, even indoor atriums. ( ③) Parks typically have trees, grass, and other plants as their central features. ( ④) When entering a city park, people often imagine a sharp separation from streets, cars, and buildings. ( ⑤) What’s behind this idea is not only landscape architects’ desire to design aesthetically suggestive park spaces, but a much longer history of Western thought that envisions cities and nature as antithetical spaces and oppositional forces.

38문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-수능 · #75
지시어 There’s a reason for that: traditionally, park designers attempted to create such a feeling by planting tall trees at park boundaries, building stone walls, and constructing other means of partition. Parks take the shape demanded by the cultural concerns of their time. Once parks are in place, they are no inert stage ― their purposes and meanings are made and remade by planners and by park users. Moments of park creation are particularly telling, 역접however, for they reveal and actualize ideas about nature and its relationship to urban society. ( ①) Indeed, what distinguishes a park from the broader category of public space is the representation of nature that parks are meant to embody. ( ②) Public spaces include parks, concrete plazas, sidewalks, even indoor atriums. ( ③) Parks typically have trees, grass, and other plants as their central features. ( ④) When entering a city park, people often imagine 핵심어a sharp separation from streets, cars, and buildings. ( ⑤) What’s behind 지시어this idea is not only landscape architects’ desire to design aesthetically suggestive park spaces, but a much longer history of Western thought that envisions cities and nature as antithetical spaces and oppositional forces.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 문장: ‘그것(that)’엔 이유가 있다 → 예전 설계자들이 ‘그런 느낌(such a feeling)’을 나무·돌담으로 만들었다 — — ‘that/such a feeling’이 가리킬 앞 문장이 반드시 필요
  2. 노랑②④ 앞: 사람들은 공원에 들어서면 거리·차·건물로부터의 ‘뚜렷한 분리(sharp separation)’를 상상한다 — — ‘such a feeling’ = 이 분리감. 주어진 문장이 이 뒤에 와야 자연스러움
  3. 노랑③⑤ 뒤: ‘이 생각(this idea)’ 뒤엔 도시와 자연을 대립으로 보는 서양 사상의 역사가 있다 — — 주어진 문장(분리를 물리적으로 구현)이 먼저 와야 ‘this idea’가 이어짐
→ ‘분리감(④)’ 뒤에 ‘그 느낌을 만든 방식(주어진 문장)’을 넣어야 ‘this idea(⑤)’로 이어진다 = ⑤
회색(공원의 문화적 형성·however 문장)은 소재 배경일 뿐, 삽입 지점 판단에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 a sharp separation → such a feeling / this idea ✓
⑤④(분리 상상) → 주어진 문장(분리를 만든 방식) → ⑤(this idea)✔ 근거
①① Indeed 문장은 앞뒤가 매끄러움✘ 없음
②② public space 예시 나열, 공백 없음✘ 없음
③③ 공원의 특징(나무·풀) 흐름 자연✘ 없음
④④ 뒤가 아니라 앞에 근거가 필요 — 여기 넣으면 ‘such a feeling’이 앞을 잃음✘ 없음

🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
When entering a city park, people often imagine a sharp separation from streets, cars, and buildings.
도시 공원에 들어설 때, 사람들은 흔히 상상한다 뚜렷한 분리를 거리·자동차·건물로부터의. ← 근거(④ 앞: such a feeling의 정체)
2
There’s a reason for that: park designers attempted to create such a feeling by planting tall trees at park boundaries, building stone walls.
거기엔 이유가 있다: 설계자들은 그런 느낌을 만들려 했다 공원 경계에 큰 나무를 심고, 돌담을 쌓아서. ← 근거(주어진 문장)

What’s behind this idea is ... a much longer history of Western thought that envisions cities and nature as antithetical spaces.
이 생각의 배후엔 더 긴 서양 사상의 역사가 있다 도시와 자연을 대립적 공간으로 보는. ← 근거(⑤: this idea로 연결)
⑤정답 ④분리감 뒤 그 느낌을 만든 방식(주어진 문장)이 와야 ‘this idea’로 연결 · ①②③④ 지시어 공백 없음

It may be easier to reach an agreement when settlement terms don’t have to be implemented until months in the future. Negotiators should try to find ways to slice a large issue into smaller pieces, known as using salami tactics. ( ① ) Issues that can be expressed in quantitative, measurable units are easy to slice. ( ② ) For example, compensation demands can be divided into cents-per-hour increments or lease rates can be quoted as dollars per square foot. ( ③ ) When working to fractionate issues of principle or precedent, parties may use the time horizon (when the principle goes into effect or how long it will last) as a way to fractionate the issue. ( ④ ) Another approach is to vary the number of ways that the principle may be applied. ( ⑤ ) For example, a company may devise a family emergency leave plan that allows employees the opportunity to be away from the company for a period of no longer than three hours, and no more than once a month, for illness in the employee’s immediate family.

39문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-수능 · #76
It may be easier to reach an agreement when settlement terms don’t have to be implemented until months in the future. Negotiators should try to find ways to slice a large issue into smaller pieces, known as using 주제salami tactics. ( ① ) Issues that can be expressed in quantitative, measurable units are easy to slice. ( ② ) For example, compensation demands can be divided into cents-per-hour increments or lease rates can be quoted as dollars per square foot. ( ③ ) When working to fractionate issues of 전환principle or precedent, parties may use the time horizon (when the principle goes into effect or how long it will last) as a way to fractionate the issue. ( ④ ) 추가Another approach is to vary the number of ways that the principle may be applied. ( ⑤ ) For example, a company may devise a family emergency leave plan that allows employees the opportunity to be away from the company for a period of no longer than three hours, and no more than once a month, for illness in the employee’s immediate family.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①주어진 문장: Another approach(또 다른 방법) = 원칙이 적용되는 방식의 수를 달리하기 — — '또 다른'은 앞에 '원칙을 쪼개는 한 방법'이 이미 있어야 성립
  2. 노랑②③ 뒤: 원칙·선례 문제는 time horizon(시간 지평)으로 쪼갠다 = 첫 번째 방법 — — 여기가 '원칙을 쪼개는 방법 ①'
  3. 노랑③⑤ For example 예시: 가족 응급 휴가제 = '적용 방식의 수'를 제한한 구체 사례 — — 주어진 문장(Another approach)을 받는 예시
→ ③(방법1) 다음에 주어진 문장(방법2)을 넣고 ⑤예시로 이어야 자연스럽다 = ④
①②의 수량화 예시는 salami 도입부라 논리에 없어도 됨.
패러프레이즈 use the time horizon (첫 방법) → Another approach (둘째 방법) ✓
①① Issues 앞✘ 없음
②② For example 앞✘ 없음
③③ When working to 앞✘ 없음
④④ Another 앞✔ 근거
⑤⑤ For example 앞✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
39문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
When working to fractionate issues of principle or precedent, parties may use the time horizon.
쪼개려고 할 때 원칙이나 선례의 문제를, 당사자는 시간 지평을 쓸 수 있다. ← ③ 앞: 방법1
2
Another approach is to vary the number of ways that the principle may be applied.
또 다른 방법은 방식의 수를 달리하는 것이다 그 원칙이 적용될 수 있는. ← 삽입 문장(방법2)
3
For example, a company may devise a family emergency leave plan for illness in the immediate family.
예를 들어, 회사는 고안할 수 있다 가족 응급 휴가제를 직계가족의 병환을 위한. ← ④ 뒤: 그 예시
④정답 ③(방법1: 시간지평) 뒤·⑤(그 예시) 앞에 'Another approach(방법2)' 삽입 · ①②③⑤논리 공백 없음

38문장 삽입 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
Science is sometimes described as a winner-take-all contest, meaning that there are no rewards for being second or third. This is an extreme view of the nature of scientific contests. (①) Even those who describe scientific contests in such a way note that it is a somewhat inaccurate description, given that replication and verification have social value and are common in science. (②) It is also inaccurate to the extent that it suggests that only a handful of contests exist. (③) But many other contests have multiple parts, and the number of such contests may be increasing. (④) By way of example, for many years it was thought that there would be “one” cure for cancer, but it is now realized that cancer takes multiple forms and that multiple approaches are needed to provide a cure. (⑤) There won’t be one winner ― there will be many.
글의 흐름으로 보아, 주어진 문장이 들어가기에 가장 적절한 곳은?
① ) Even those who describe scientific contests in such a② ) It is also inaccurate to the extent③ ) But④ ) By way of example,⑤ ) There won’t be one winner ― there will be many.

지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
38문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2024-수능 · #77
Science is sometimes described as a winner-take-all contest, meaning that there are no rewards for being second or third. This is an extreme view of the nature of scientific contests. (①) Even those who describe scientific contests in such a way note that it is a somewhat inaccurate description, given that replication and verification have social value and are common in science. (②) It is also inaccurate to the extent that it suggests that 한정only a handful of contests exist. (③) 역접But many other contests have multiple parts, and the number of such contests may be increasing. (④) By way of example, for many years it was thought that there would be “one” cure for cancer, but it is now realized that cancer takes multiple forms and that multiple approaches are needed to provide a cure. (⑤) There won’t be one winner ― there will be many.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①삽입 문장: ‘그래(Yes), 어떤 경쟁은 세계적 수준’ (힉스·초전도 예) — — ‘소수의 경쟁만 존재’를 인정하는 문장
  2. 노랑②② 앞: 승자독식이 ‘소수(only a handful)만 존재’ 시사라 부정확 — — 세계적 경쟁 = 그 소수를 예로 받음
  3. 노랑③③ ‘But 다른 많은 경쟁은 여러 부분으로’ → 소수 언급이 앞에 있어야 대조 성립 — — 논리 공백: But 앞에 ‘소수’ 확인 문장 필요
→ ‘Yes, 세계적 경쟁(소수)’ 다음 ‘But 많은 다른 경쟁’ = 삽입 위치 ③
①·⑤(회색)은 흐름 유지, 암 예시(회색)는 부연.
패러프레이즈 only a handful of contests exist → Yes, some contests are seen as world class (③) ✓
③③ (‘only a handful’ ↔ ‘But many other’ 사이)✔ 근거
①① (부정확하다는 부연 앞)✘ 없음
②② (‘only a handful’ 문장 앞)✘ 없음
④④ (암 치료 예시 앞)✘ 없음
⑤⑤ (마지막 결론 앞)✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
38문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
It is also inaccurate to the extent that it suggests that only a handful of contests exist.
그것은 또한 부정확하다 …을 시사한다는 점에서 오직 소수의 경쟁만 존재한다고. ← ③앞
2
But many other contests have multiple parts, and the number of such contests may be increasing.
그러나 다른 많은 경쟁은 여러 부분을 가지며, 그런 경쟁의 수는 늘고 있을지 모른다. ← ③뒤
③정답 ‘소수만 존재’와 ‘But 많은 다른 경쟁’ 사이 · ① ② ④ ⑤ 모두 논리 공백 없음
wbspread
38문장 삽입 2점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
As winter approaches, the length of the day shortens, the temperature drops, and plants, including trees, can detect this change. It is worth pointing out that leaves don’t drop to the ground because they are dying ― rather, the tree initiates an active process of clever recycling called senescence. A tree, like an oak for example, would struggle to survive through a harsh winter if it retained its canopy of leaves. ( ①) It would risk damage from strong winter winds and would lose more water from its leaves than it could draw up from the frozen ground. ( ②) If it didn’t blow over, it would die of thirst. ( ③) It signals to them that it is time to lose their leaves. ( ④) First, however, trees carefully suck all of the useful nutrients out of the leaves and then, with surgical precision, block up that pathway into the leaves. ( ⑤) That blocked pathway at the base of the leaf stem creates a weakness and, in the wind, the leaves snap off and fall to the ground.
글의 흐름으로 보아, 주어진 문장이 들어가기에 가장 적절한 곳은?
① ) It would risk② ) If it didn’t blow over, it would die of thirst. (③ ) It④ ) First,⑤ ) That blocked pathway at the

  • 지문 단어 그대로 쓴 선지부터 지웠나?

38문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2026-09 · #78
As winter approaches, the length of the day shortens, the temperature drops, and plants, including trees, can detect 선행어this change. It is worth pointing out that leaves don’t drop to the ground because they are dying ― 대조rather, the tree initiates an active process of clever recycling called senescence. A tree, like an oak for example, would struggle to survive through a harsh winter if it retained its canopy of leaves. ( ①) It would risk damage from strong winter winds and would lose more water from its leaves than it could draw up from the frozen ground. ( ②) 조건If it didn’t blow over, it would die of thirst. 지시어 ( ③) It signals to them that it is time to lose their leaves. ( ④) 순서·역접First, however, trees carefully suck all of the useful nutrients out of the leaves and then, with surgical precision, block up that pathway into the leaves. ( ⑤) 지시어That blocked pathway at the base of the leaf stem creates a weakness and, in the wind, the leaves snap off and fall to the ground.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①삽입 문장: 'It signals to them' → It=서두의 '이 변화(this change)', them=나무들 — — 대명사 It·them의 선행어는 서두의 '변화'와 '나무'뿐
  2. 노랑②②(목마름으로 죽음)까지는 '잎을 유지하면 위험'하다는 이야기 → 잎을 떨어뜨릴 때라는 언급이 아직 없음 — — ②와 ④ 사이에 '잎을 떨어뜨린다'는 화제 도입이 비어 있음
  3. 노랑③④ First, however, 나무는 잎에서 양분을 빨아들이고… = 잎을 떨어뜨리는 '과정의 시작' — — '먼저(First)'가 성립하려면 앞에 '잎을 떨어뜨림'이 도입돼야 함
→ ③ 자리에 '잎을 떨어뜨릴 때라는 신호'를 넣어야 ②(위험)→③(신호)→④(과정 시작)로 연결 = ③
①②(바람·목마름 위험)와 ⑤(떨어지는 결과)는 삽입 지점 판정의 배경일 뿐.
패러프레이즈 this change · trees → It · them(삽입 문장의 대명사) ✓
③② die of thirst 〔③〕 First, however … → '신호' 문장 삽입✔ 근거
①canopy 유지 위험 〔①〕 It would risk damage✘ 없음
②① winds·water 〔②〕 If it didn’t blow over✘ 없음
④③ 신호 〔④〕 First, however (이미 연결됨)✘ 없음
⑤④ 통로 차단 〔⑤〕 That blocked pathway✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
38문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
As winter approaches, the length of the day shortens, the temperature drops, and plants, including trees, can detect this change.
겨울이 다가오면서, 낮이 짧아지고 기온이 떨어지며, 나무를 포함한 식물들은 이 변화를 감지할 수 있다. ← It·them의 선행어
2
It signals to them that it is time to lose their leaves.
그것(변화)은 그들(나무)에게 신호를 보낸다 때가 되었다고 잎을 떨어뜨릴. ← 근거(삽입 문장 ③)
3
First, however, trees carefully suck all of the useful nutrients out of the leaves and then, with surgical precision, block up that pathway into the leaves.
그러나 먼저, 나무는 잎에서 유용한 양분을 모두 조심스럽게 빨아들이고 그다음 외과적 정밀함으로, 잎으로 통하는 그 통로를 막는다. ← '잎을 떨어뜨리는 과정'의 시작
③정답 ②(위험)→〔신호: 잎 떨굴 때〕→④(First 과정 시작)로 논리 공백 메움 · ①②④⑤ 대명사·순서가 이미 자연스러워 공백 없음

38문장 삽입 STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
To minimize vortex shedding, skyscraper designers do everything in their powers to confuse the wind. To minimize building costs and maximize revenues, skyscrapers generally take the shape of squares or rectangles. These are not particularly aerodynamic shapes, and this results in a wind phenomenon called “vortex shedding.” As wind meets a rectangular skyscraper it pushes on the flat face of the building before flowing around its sides, where eventually it separates from the face of the structure. ( ① ) The difference in pressures on the front and back faces of the building gives rise to vortices, or spinning currents of wind, that flow downstream from the building. ( ② ) The vortices pull and push the building in a direction perpendicular to the wind at frequencies that can become dangerously self-sustaining. ( ③ ) Orienting the building so that the longer face of the structure is parallel with prevailing winds can help. ( ④ ) Chopping or rounding off corners of a building can likewise make it more aerodynamic. ( ⑤ ) Roughing up the corners of the building through the careful placement of balconies or stepped corners can help by disturbing or delaying the formation of strong vortices.
글의 흐름으로 보아, 주어진 문장이 들어가기에 가장 적절한 곳은?
① ) The difference in② ) The vortices pull and push the③ ) Orienting④ ) Chopping or rounding off⑤ ) Roughing up the corners of the building through the

삽입문장To minimize vortex shedding, skyscraper designers do everything in their powers to confuse the wind. To minimize building costs and maximize revenues, skyscrapers generally take the shape of squares or rectangles. 지시어These are not particularly aerodynamic shapes, and this results in a wind phenomenon called “vortex shedding.” As wind meets a rectangular skyscraper it pushes on the flat face of the building before flowing around its sides, where eventually it separates from the face of the structure. ( ① ) 정관사The difference in pressures on the front and back faces of the building gives rise to vortices, or spinning currents of wind, that flow downstream from the building. ( ② ) 지시어The vortices pull and push the building in a direction perpendicular to the wind at frequencies that can become dangerously self-sustaining. ( ③ ) Orienting the building so that the longer face of the structure is parallel with prevailing winds can help. ( ⑤ ) Roughing up the corners of the building through the careful placement of balconies or stepped corners can help by disturbing or delaying the formation of strong vortices.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①삽입문장 = ‘소용돌이 흘림(vortex shedding)을 최소화하려 설계자들이 바람을 교란시킨다’는 주제 문장 — — 바람을 다루는 설계 원리를 먼저 제시
  2. 노랑②① 앞: 바람이 건물 면을 지나 분리된다 / ① 뒤: ‘그 압력 차(The difference)’가 소용돌이를 만든다 — — 주제(바람 교란) 문장이 먼저 들어가고, 그 뒤로 소용돌이의 발생·위험 설명이 이어짐
→ 바람을 교란시킨다는 주제 문장은 ① 자리에 들어간다
도입·예시(회색)는 논리에 영향 없음.
패러프레이즈 confuse the wind → ① 앞 위치 ✓
①① The difference in pressures 앞✔ 근거
②② The vortices pull and push 앞✘ 없음
③③ Orienting 앞✘ 없음
④④ Chopping or rounding off 앞✘ 없음
⑤⑤ Roughing up the corners 앞✘ 없음

🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
To minimize vortex shedding, skyscraper designers do everything in their powers to confuse the wind.
소용돌이 흘림을 최소화하려고, 초고층 설계자들은 온갖 수단을 다한다 바람을 교란시키기 위해. ← 삽입문장
2
The difference in pressures on the front and back faces of the building gives rise to vortices.
압력의 차이가 건물 앞·뒷면의 소용돌이를 일으킨다. ← ① 뒤 연결
①정답 바람 교란이라는 주제 문장이 압력차·소용돌이 설명 앞에 · ②③④⑤

39문장 삽입 3점STEP 1 · 직접 풀기 ✍️
🖍 신호 사전을 떠올리며 무조건 읽을 문장에 형광펜을 직접 치고, 예시는 넘기며 답을 골라보세요.
The dynamics of collective detection have an interesting feature. Which cue(s) do individuals use as evidence of predator attack? In some cases, when an individual detects a predator, its best response is to seek shelter. ( ①) Departure from the group may signal danger to nonvigilant animals and cause what appears to be a coordinated flushing of prey from the area. ( ②) Studies on dark-eyed juncos support the view that nonvigilant animals attend to departures of individual group mates but that the departure of multiple individuals causes a greater escape response. [삽입문] This makes sense from the perspective of information reliability. ( ③) If one group member departs, it might have done so for a number of reasons that have little to do with predation threat. ( ④) If nonvigilant animals escaped each time a single member left the group, they would frequently respond when there was no predator (a false alarm). ( ⑤) On the other hand, when several individuals depart the group at the same time, a true threat is much more likely to be present.
글의 흐름으로 보아, 주어진 문장이 들어가기에 가장 적절한 곳은?
① ) Departure② ) Studies on dark-eyed juncos (a type of③ ) If one group member departs,④ ) If nonvigilant animals escaped⑤ ) On the other hand, when several individuals

39문장 삽입STEP 2 · 훈련 (정답 칠)2023-06 · #80
The dynamics of collective detection have an interesting feature. 질문Which cue(s) do individuals use as evidence of predator attack? In some cases, when an individual detects a predator, its best response is to seek shelter. ( ①) Departure from the group may signal danger to nonvigilant animals and cause what appears to be a coordinated flushing of prey from the area. ( ②) Studies on dark-eyed juncos support the view that nonvigilant animals attend to departures of individual group mates 대조but that the departure of multiple individuals causes a greater escape response. [삽입문] 지시This makes sense from the perspective of information reliability. ( ③) 조건If one group member departs, it might have done so for a number of reasons that have little to do with predation threat. ( ④) 조건If nonvigilant animals escaped each time a single member left the group, they would frequently respond when there was no predator (a false alarm). ( ⑤) 역접On the other hand, when several individuals depart the group at the same time, a true threat is much more likely to be present.
🟡 노란색 문장만으로 정답이 나오는 과정 (지문 속 노란 라벨 = 그 문장을 칠한 이유·신호)
  1. 노랑①②까지: 여럿이 떠나면 더 큰 도피 반응(연구 결과 제시) — — 개체 수에 따라 반응이 달라진다는 사실
  2. 노랑②삽입문 'This는 정보 신뢰성 관점에서 말이 된다' — — This가 받을 앞 내용이 있어야 함
  3. 노랑③③ 이후: 한 마리만 떠나면 포식과 무관할 수 있다 → 왜 신뢰성 관점인지 설명 시작 — — 삽입문 뒤에 근거 설명이 이어짐
→ 연구 결과(②) 뒤에 '이는 정보 신뢰성상 타당' 삽입, 이후 ③이 이유를 풀어감 = ③
①(집단 이탈=위험 신호)은 배경, 논리 공백은 ③ 앞에 발생.
패러프레이즈 greater escape response for multiple departures→This makes sense (reliability) → ③ ✓
③③ 위치✔ 근거
①① 위치✘ 없음
②② 위치✘ 없음
④④ 위치✘ 없음
⑤⑤ 위치✘ 없음
공식지시어가 갑자기 튀는 '논리 공백' 지점
39문장 삽입STEP 3 · 해석 (직독직해)🟡문장·선지만
🟡 무조건 읽는 문장 — 같은 형광펜 색끼리 영↔한 대응
1
This makes sense from the perspective of information reliability.
이것은 타당하다 정보 신뢰성의 관점에서. ← 삽입문: This=앞의 연구 결과
2
If one group member departs, it might have done so for a number of reasons that have little to do with predation threat.
한 구성원이 떠난다면, 그것은 여러 이유로 그랬을 수 있다 포식 위협과 별 관계 없는. ← ③ 뒤: 신뢰성 낮은 이유 설명
3
On the other hand, when several individuals depart at the same time, a true threat is much more likely to be present.
반면, 여럿이 동시에 떠날 때는, 진짜 위협이 존재할 가능성이 훨씬 크다. ← 대조 마무리
③정답 삽입문 This=②의 연구 결과 · 이후 ③④가 '정보 신뢰성'을 구체적으로 설명
